## 1400000
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "舞踏会ＶＯＬ４用メッセージ開始<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "무도회 VOL4용 메시지 시작<end>"

## 1400001
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これは<value:$28>様。<line-break>今日はどちらへおいでですか？<end>"
EN: "Ah, <value:$28>. Where are you headed today?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님이시군요. 오늘은 어디로 가십니까?<end>"

## 1400002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "別に用はない<end>"
EN: "Nothing in particular<end>"
KO: "딱히 볼일은 없다<end>"

## 1400003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ王女の部屋<end>"
EN: "Princess Tiana's Room<end>"
KO: "티아나 왕녀의 방<end>"

## 1400004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エリス王妃の執務室<end>"
EN: "Queen Eris's Office<end>"
KO: "에리스 왕비의 집무실<end>"

## 1400005
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "アトレイア王女の部屋<end>"
EN: "Princess Atleia's Room<end>"
KO: "아트레이아 왕녀의 방<end>"

## 1400006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか。　　　　　　　　<line-break> <end>"
EN: "I see.<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까.<end>"

## 1400007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうぞ、お通りください。　　<line-break> <end>"
EN: "Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "어서 지나가십시오.<end>"

## 1400031
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これで強くなれるんだな。<end>"
EN: "…So this will make me stronger.<end>"
KO: "…이제 강해질 수 있는 거군.<end>"

## 1400032
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まーかせちゃってよ。<end>"
EN: "Just leave it to me!<end>"
KO: "나한테 맡겨.<end>"

## 1400033
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテスめ。クズのくせに<line-break>この俺様をコケにしやがって…。<line-break>これで、たたきのめしてやる…！<end>"
EN: "Damn Xenetes. Worthless scum, making a fool of me… Now I'll crush him…!<end>"
KO: "제네테스 녀석. 쓰레기 주제에 이 나님을 우습게 봐…. 이걸로 때려눕혀 주마…!<end>"

## 1400034
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ティアナ様なんかが見ている前で<line-break>のしちゃった方が効果的じゃないかな。<line-break>その舞台もちゃんと用意してあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Wouldn't it be more effective to crush him with Lady Tiana watching? I'll make sure the stage is set for you.<end>"
KO: "티아나 님 같은 사람이 보는 앞에서 때려눕히는 편이 더 효과적이지 않을까? 그 무대도 제대로 준비해 줄게.<end>"

## 1400035
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺に指図するな。<end>"
EN: "…Don't tell me what to do.<end>"
KO: "…나한테 명령하지 마.<end>"

## 1400036
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くすっ。<line-break>なにを恐れているんだい？<end>"
EN: "Heh. What are you afraid of?<end>"
KO: "후훗. 뭘 두려워하는 거지?<end>"

## 1400037
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うるさい！<line-break>絶対、ゼネテスをたたきのめす！<line-break>それでいいのだろうが！　クズめ！<end>"
EN: "Shut up! I'll crush Xenetes, no matter what! That's what you want, isn't it? You scum!<end>"
KO: "시끄러워! 반드시 제네테스를 때려눕힐 거야! 그러면 되는 거잖아! 쓰레기야!<end>"

## 1400038
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "タルテュバ、君の痛みはわかるよ。<line-break>だから、ついでに<line-break>願いをかなえてあげるのさ。<end>"
EN: "Tartuba, I understand your pain. So while I'm at it, I'll grant your wish too.<end>"
KO: "타르튜바, 네 아픔은 알아. 그러니까 덤으로 소원을 이루어 주는 거야.<end>"

## 1400040
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "王妃様からお話は伺っています。<line-break>どうぞ、お通りください。<end>"
EN: "Her Majesty has told me about you. Please, go on through.<end>"
KO: "왕비님께 말씀 들었습니다. 들어가십시오.<end>"

## 1400041
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…これで強くなれるんだな。<end>"
EN: "…So this will make me stronger.<end>"
KO: "…이제 강해질 수 있는 거군.<end>"

## 1400042

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まーかせちゃってよ。<end>"
EN: "Just leave it to me!<end>"
KO: "나한테 맡겨.<end>"

## 1400049
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（あれは、いったい…？）<end>"
EN: "(What in the world was that...?)<end>"
KO: "(저건 대체…?)<end>"

## 1400050
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よ、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんな手紙が来た。<line-break>読んでみてくれ。<end>"
EN: "Hey, <value:$28>. I got this letter. Have a read.<end>"
KO: "여어, <value:$28>. 이런 편지가 왔어. 읽어봐.<end>"

## 1400051
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様をお慕いもうしております。<line-break>空中庭園でお待ちしております。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　かしこ　　<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes, I adore you. I will be waiting in the Hanging Gardens. Respectfully yours,<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님을 사모하고 있습니다. 공중정원에서 기다리겠습니다. 삼가.<end>"

## 1400052
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うさんくささがバクハツしてるだろ？<line-break>これ作った奴は俺のことが<line-break>よくわかってるよ。<line-break>これほど、あざとくも稚拙な<line-break>おびき寄せの罠なんて、<line-break>俺にとっては挑戦状みたいなもんだ。<end>"
EN: "This thing couldn't look more suspicious if it tried, could it? Whoever made it really knows me. A trap this transparent and amateurish is practically a challenge to me.<end>"
KO: "수상쩍음이 폭발하지? 이걸 만든 녀석은 나를 잘 알아. 이렇게 노골적이면서 서툰 유인 함정은 내게 도전장이나 다름없어.<end>"

## 1400053
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…てなわけで、これから、ちょっくら、<line-break>危険に足をつっこみに行こうと<line-break>思うわけだが、一緒に来るよな？<end>"
EN: "So anyway, I'm off to wade into a little danger. You're coming with me, right?<end>"
KO: "…그런 이유로 이제 좀 위험한 곳에 발을 들이밀러 가려는데, 같이 올 거지?<end>"

## 1400054
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行く<end>"
EN: "Go<end>"
KO: "간다<end>"

## 1400055
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行かない<end>"
EN: "Don't go<end>"
KO: "안 간다<end>"

## 1400056
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ひとつだけ、警告しておく。<line-break>スリルは中毒になりやすい。<line-break>お互い、気をつけないとな。<line-break>ふふ、だが、そうこなくっちゃ、<line-break>人生おもしろく生きられない。<line-break>んじゃ、ま、いくぜ。<end>"
EN: "Just one warning: thrills can be addictive. We'd better both be careful. Heh. But without a little danger, life's no fun. Well then, let's go.<end>"
KO: "…한 가지만 경고해 두지. 스릴은 중독되기 쉬워. 서로 조심해야겠어. 후후, 하지만 그래야 인생이 재미있지. 그럼, 가 보자.<end>"

## 1400057
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空中庭園まで来たのはいいが…。<line-break>な～んにも、ねえな。<line-break>すごい悪のニオイがしたのにな。<end>"
EN: "Made it to the Hanging Gardens, but... there's nothing here. Could've sworn I smelled something really evil.<end>"
KO: "공중정원까지 오긴 했는데…. 아~무것도 없네. 엄청난 악의 냄새가 났는데 말이지.<end>"

## 1400058
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス？<end>"
EN: "...Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "…제네테스?<end>"

## 1400059
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "レムオン？<line-break>こりゃおもしろい。<line-break>おい、お前がこの手紙の主か？<end>"
EN: "Lemghon? Well, this is interesting. Hey, are you the one who sent this letter?<end>"
KO: "레무온? 이거 재미있군. 이봐, 네가 이 편지의 주인이냐?<end>"

## 1400060
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だっ誰が、貴様などに<line-break>ラブレターを出すものか！！<end>"
EN: "W-Who would ever send the likes of you a love letter?!<end>"
KO: "누, 누가 네놈 따위에게 러브레터를 보낸다는 거냐!!<end>"

## 1400061
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だ、エリエナイ公。<line-break>そんなに怒ってちゃダメだぜ。<line-break>もっと、人生楽しまないとな。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking, Duke Elienai. Don't get so worked up. You ought to enjoy life a little more.<end>"
KO: "농담이야, 에리에나이 공. 그렇게 화내면 안 되지. 인생을 좀 더 즐겨야 한다고.<end>"

## 1400062
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…それよりも、ファーロス総司令。<line-break>俺の妹に手を出すのは<line-break>やめてもらおうか。<end>"
EN: "More importantly, Commander-in-Chief Pharos, keep your hands off my sister.<end>"
KO: "…그보다 파로스 총사령관. 내 여동생에게 손대는 건 그만두시지.<end>"

## 1400063
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お義兄さま！<line-break>そんなつれないことを<line-break>おっしゃらないでください！<end>"
EN: "Brother-in-law! Please don't be so cold!<end>"
KO: "형부님! 그렇게 매정한 말씀은 하지 말아 주세요!<end>"

## 1400064
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰が、貴様のお義兄さまだ！<end>"
EN: "Who are you calling your brother-in-law?!<end>"
KO: "누가 네 형님이냐!<end>"

## 1400065
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…我が弟、<value:$28>よ。<line-break>そんなバカのそばに寄るんじゃない。<line-break>バカとなまけぐせがうつるぞ。<end>"
EN: "My younger brother, <value:$28>... Stay away from that fool. The stupidity and laziness might rub off on you.<end>"
KO: "…내 동생 <value:$28>아. 그런 바보 곁에 가지 마라. 바보와 게으름이 옮는다.<end>"

## 1400066
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だとよ。じゃあ、<line-break>エリエナイ公のそばに寄ると<line-break>傲慢と仕事中毒がうつるぜ？<end>"
EN: "You heard him. So if you get too close to Duke Elienai, will the arrogance and workaholism rub off on you?<end>"
KO: "…그렇다네. 그럼 엘리에나이 공 옆에 가면 오만함과 일중독이 옮겠군?<end>"

## 1400067
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴のそばにいると<line-break>その上、女癖の悪さもうつる。<end>"
EN: "Hang around him and his womanizing will rub off on you too.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석 곁에 있으면 여자 버릇 나쁜 것까지 옮는다.<end>"

## 1400068
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、エリエナイ公のそばにいると<line-break>女っ気のない、仕事仕事の<line-break>多忙で灰色の毎日がうつりそうだな。<end>"
EN: "Then stay around Duke Elienai and you'll end up with no women in your life, nothing but work, and one gray, busy day after another.<end>"
KO: "그럼 에리에나이 공 곁에 있으면 여자 기운 하나 없는, 일뿐인 바쁘고 잿빛인 나날이 옮아붙겠네.<end>"

## 1400069
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、うるさい！<end>"
EN: "S-Shut up!<end>"
KO: "시, 시끄러워!<end>"

## 1400070
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だ、エリエナイ公。<line-break>どうやら、お前さんも<line-break>おびき寄せられたようだな。<end>"
EN: "Only joking, Duke Elienai. Looks like you were lured here too.<end>"
KO: "농담이다, 에리에나이 공. 아무래도 자네도유인당한 모양이군.<end>"

## 1400071
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28> 님.<end>"

## 1400072
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<line-break>私、手紙をちゃんと<line-break>自分で読んでまいりました！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! I read the letter properly, all by myself!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님! 저, 편지를 제대로 제 손으로 읽고 왔습니다!<end>"

## 1400073
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロス総司令。<line-break>どういうおつもりなのです？<line-break>こんな所に呼びだすなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief Pharos, what is the meaning of this? Summoning me to a place like this...<end>"
KO: "파로스 총사령관. 무슨 생각이십니까? 이런 곳으로 불러내다니….<end>"

## 1400074
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様？<line-break>それにレムオン様も…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...? And Lemghon as well...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님? 레무온 님도….<end>"

## 1400075
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテスに呼びだされた、だと？<end>"
EN: "You mean Xenetes summoned you here...?<end>"
KO: "…제네테스가 불렀다고?<end>"

## 1400076
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ってことは、お前さんは<line-break>ティアナに呼びだされたわけか。<line-break>フフ、かわいいとこがあるもんだな。<end>"
EN: "So Tiana was the one who summoned you. Heh. That's rather sweet.<end>"
KO: "…그럼 너는 티아나에게 불려 온 거구나. 후후, 귀여운 면도 있네.<end>"

## 1400077
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が言いたい！<end>"
EN: "What are you trying to say?!<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 하고 싶은 거야!<end>"

## 1400078
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、さっきから言ってるだろ？<line-break>俺たちは全員、<line-break>罠にハメられたんだって<line-break>そら、今回の罠の<line-break>仕掛け人のご登場だ。<line-break>詳しいことは奴から聞いてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I've been telling you all along, haven't I? We've all been caught in a trap. Look, here comes the one who set it. Get the details straight from the source.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 아까부터 말했잖아? 우리 모두가, 함정에 빠진 거라고. 자, 이번 함정의장본인이 등장했군. 자세한 건 녀석에게 물어봐.<end>"

## 1400079
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフフッ。<line-break>ようこそ、みなさん！<line-break>闇の王女のための血の晩餐会へ！<end>"
EN: "Heh heh heh. Welcome, one and all, to a banquet of blood in honor of the Princess of Darkness!<end>"
KO: "후후훗. 어서 오세요, 여러분! 어둠의 왕녀를 위한 피의 만찬회에!<end>"

## 1400080
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お前は、シャリ！？<line-break>道化め！　どういうつもりだ？<end>"
EN: "Shalli!? You damned jester! What are you playing at?<end>"
KO: "너는 샤리!? 광대 녀석! 무슨 속셈이지?<end>"

## 1400081
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、道化か…。<line-break>道化ってのはね、神の用意した運命に<line-break>翻弄される君たちの方だよ。<line-break>僕が、そのこっけいすぎる運命を<line-break>完璧に終わらせてあげるよ。<line-break>闇の王女とその神によってね。<end>"
EN: "Heh. A jester, am I? No, the jesters are you, tossed about by the fate your god has laid out for you. I'll bring that absurd fate to a perfect end through the Princess of Darkness and her god.<end>"
KO: "후후, 광대라…. 광대라는 건 신이 마련한 운명에 휘둘리는 너희 쪽이야. 내가 그 우스꽝스러운 운명을 완벽하게 끝내 줄게. 어둠의 왕녀와 그 신으로 말이지.<end>"

## 1400082
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "気に入らねえゴタクだな。<line-break>何様のつもりだ？<end>"
EN: "I don't like that pompous drivel. Who the hell do you think you are?<end>"
KO: "마음에 안 드는 소리군. 네가 뭐라도 되는 줄 알아?<end>"

## 1400083
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕は、破壊神の復活を目論む秘密結社、<line-break>システィーナの伝道師のひとり、<line-break>東方の博士、シャリ。<line-break>このお城のすべての人間の命を<line-break>闇の王女のための血のいけにえに<line-break>捧げるにあたって…<end>"
EN: "I am Shalli, the Scholar from the East and one of the missionaries of Sistina, a secret society plotting the resurrection of the God of Destruction. And now, as I offer every life in this castle as a blood sacrifice to the Princess of Darkness...<end>"
KO: "나는 파괴신 부활을 꾀하는 비밀결사 시스티나의 전도사 중 하나, 동방의 박사 샤리. 이 성의 모든 사람 목숨을 어둠의 왕녀를 위한 피의 제물로 바치면서….<end>"

## 1400084
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス、そしてレムオン。<line-break>君たち、闇の王女の神に刃向かう<line-break>運命を秘めたる者たち…<line-break>そして、<value:$28>、<line-break>無限のソウルの資質を持つ者を、<line-break>第一に、捧げよう。フフフ。<end>"
EN: "Xenetes and Lemghon, you who carry within you a destiny to defy the god of the Princess of Darkness... And <value:$28>, who bears the potential of the Infinite Soul. You will be the first I offer up. Heh heh heh.<end>"
KO: "제네테스, 그리고 레무온. 너희, 어둠의 왕녀의 신에게 맞설 운명을 품은 자들… 그리고 <value:$28>, 무한의 소울의 자질을 지닌 자를 가장 먼저 바치겠다. 후후후.<end>"

## 1400085
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "出ておいで、タルテュバ。<line-break>君の願い、かなえてあげるよ。<line-break>君の嫉妬心を力に変えてあげる。<line-break>心の闇を解放して怪物になりなよ。<line-break>そして、憎いゼネテスとレムオンを<line-break>ティアナの前で八つ裂きにするといい。<end>"
EN: "Come forth, Tartuba. I'll grant your wish. I'll turn your jealousy into power. Unleash the darkness in your heart and become a monster. Then tear the hated Xenetes and Lemghon limb from limb before Tiana's eyes.<end>"
KO: "나와, 타르튜바. 네 소원을 이뤄 줄게. 네 질투심을 힘으로 바꿔 주지. 마음의 어둠을 풀어 괴물이 되어. 그리고 미운 제네테스와 레무온을 티아나 앞에서 갈기갈기 찢어 버려.<end>"

## 1400086
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "くっ、くっ、クズめ…クズめ、クズめ！<line-break>俺が、俺が英雄になるべきだったのに…<line-break>どいつも、俺のことを認めないで…！<end>"
EN: "S-scum... Scum, scum! I―I was supposed to be the hero... But none of them would ever acknowledge me...!<end>"
KO: "크, 크윽, 쓰레기 자식… 쓰레기, 쓰레기! 내가, 내가 영웅이 되었어야 하는데… 아무도 나를 인정하지 않고…!<end>"

## 1400087
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…お前さんのことを一番認めてないのは<line-break>お前さん自身だ。そして、<line-break>一番に認めてやるべきだったのもな。<line-break>完璧でなくても認めてやるべきだった。<line-break>自分であることすら恐れている奴が<line-break>英雄なんかには、なれやしない。<end>"
EN: "The one who accepts you least is you. And you were the one who should've accepted yourself before anyone else. You should've accepted yourself even if you weren't perfect. You can never become a hero when you're afraid even to be yourself.<end>"
KO: "…너를 가장 인정하지 않는 건 너 자신이야. 그리고 가장 먼저 인정해 줬어야 하는 것도 너 자신이지. 완벽하지 않아도 인정했어야 했다. 자기 자신인 것조차 두려워하는 놈이 영웅이 될 수 있겠냐.<end>"

## 1400088
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "俺のことをいつもバカにしやがって…！<line-break>全部、全部、全部！　お前のせいだ！<line-break>ぶっ殺す！　ぶっ殺す！　ぶっ殺す！！<end>"
EN: "You were always making a fool of me...! Everything―everything, everything―is your fault! I'll kill you! Kill you! Kill you!!<end>"
KO: "늘 나를 바보 취급하고…! 전부, 전부, 전부! 다 네 탓이야! 죽여 버리겠어! 죽여 버리겠어! 죽여 버리겠어!!<end>"

## 1400089
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ヤバそうだね、こりゃ。<line-break>理性も何もトんじまってる。<line-break>俺が目当てだったことも忘れてるな。<line-break>…みんな逃げろ！<line-break>ここは俺と<value:$28>で<line-break>なんとかする！<end>"
EN: "This looks bad. There's no reason left in him at all. He's even forgotten I was his target. Everyone, run! <value:$28> and I will handle this!<end>"
KO: "이거 위험해 보이는데. 이성이고 뭐고 다 날아갔어. 내가 목적이었던 것도 잊었군. …다들 도망쳐! 여긴 나와 <value:$28>가 어떻게든 할게!<end>"

## 1400090
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml

KO: "<value:$28>님!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス様！<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes...!<end>"

## 1400095
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "彼は、アトレイア王女をかばったねえ。<line-break>そのことと、そのいい感じの<line-break>嫉妬の表情は関係あるのかな？<end>"
EN: "Oh, he shielded Princess Atleia. I wonder if that has anything to do with that delightful look of jealousy.<end>"
KO: "그는 아트레이아 왕녀를 감쌌지. 그 일과 그 보기 좋은 질투하는 표정은 관계가 있는 걸까?<end>"

## 1400096
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いい子だ…、<line-break>こっちだぜ…、ほら…。<end>"
EN: "That's it... Over here... Come on...<end>"
KO: "착하지… 이리 와… 자….<end>"

## 1400097
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その上、命がけで<line-break>タルテュバの注意を引きつけてるよ。<line-break>やっぱ違うね、ホンモノの王女様は。<line-break>一方の君は誰にも相手にされない<line-break>偽りの光の王女…くすっ。<line-break>闇の王女の名は君にこそふさわしい。<end>"
EN: "And now he's risking his life to keep Tartuba's attention. A true princess really is different. While you―the false Princess of Light―are ignored by everyone. Heh. You're the one who truly deserves the name Princess of Darkness.<end>"
KO: "게다가 목숨을 걸고 타르튜바의 주의를 끌고 있잖아. 역시 진짜 왕녀는 다르네. 반면 넌 누구에게도 상대받지 못하는 가짜 빛의 왕녀…. 후후. 어둠의 왕녀라는 이름은 너야말로 어울려.<end>"

## 1400098
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "へへ…、思ったより<line-break>いいパンチをもらっちまったな…。<line-break>カッコ悪いが、動けねぇ…。<end>"
EN: "Heh... That hit was harder than I expected. Pretty pathetic, but I can't move...<end>"
KO: "헤헤… 생각보다 제대로 한 방 먹었네…. 꼴사납지만 움직일 수가 없어….<end>"

## 1400099
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "来いよ…。タルテュバ…。<line-break>命と引き替えに、とびっきりの<line-break>カウンターを決めてやるぜ…。<end>"
EN: "Come on, Tartuba... I'll trade my life for one hell of a counterblow...<end>"
KO: "와라…. 타르튜바…. 목숨을 걸고 끝내주는 카운터를 먹여 주지….<end>"

## 1400100

JP: "いやあぁああ！<end>"
EN: "Nooooo!<end>"
KO: "싫어어어어!<end>"

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400103
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "그 힘…? …후후, 그런 거였나. 오늘은 신월이었지.<end>"

## 1400106
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400107
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いっそ死んでくれればありがたいが<line-break>そうもいかないようだ。<line-break>しぶといだけが取り柄の役立たずめ！<end>"
EN: "I'd be grateful if he simply died, but apparently I won't be so fortunate. Useless fool―stubbornness is his only virtue!<end>"
KO: "차라리 죽어 주면 고맙겠지만 그렇게는 안 되는 모양이군. 질긴 것밖에 장점 없는 쓸모없는 녀석!<end>"

## 1400108
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…すまねえ。<end>"

KO: "…미안하다.<end>"

## 1400109
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あの、とにかく、<line-break>すぐに人を呼んできます！<end>"
EN: "Um... Anyway, I'll go get help right away!<end>"
KO: "저, 저기, 아무튼 곧 사람을 불러올게요!<end>"

## 1400110
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あわてるほどのことか。<line-break>死にはしない。<end>"
EN: "There is no need for such panic. He won't die.<end>"
KO: "그렇게 서두를 일인가. 죽지는 않아.<end>"

## 1400111
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…甘っちょろい冒険者め！<line-break>これが私の破滅につながるのだぞ…！<end>"
EN: "You softhearted adventurer! This could be my undoing...!<end>"
KO: "…물러터진 모험자 같으니! 이게 내 파멸로 이어질 거라고…!<end>"

## 1400112
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…このことを知った叔母貴が動き出す。<line-break>やっこさんの秘密を守れるのは、<line-break>お前さんしか…いない。<end>"
EN: "Once my aunt hears about this, she'll make her move. You're the only one who can keep that secret safe...<end>"
KO: "…이 사실을 알게 된 아주머니가 움직일 거야. 그 사람의 비밀을 지킬 수 있는 건 너밖에… 없어.<end>"

## 1400113
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Come on, Tartuba... That's it, over here. Get away from the princess...<end>"
KO: "이리 와…. 타르튜바…. 자, 이쪽이다. 공주님에게서 떨어져….<end>"

## 1400114

## 1400115
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400117
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400119
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…俺も倒れるほどがんばったのにさ。<line-break>俺には感謝のコトバとか、なしなの？<end>"
EN: "I worked so hard I collapsed too, you know. Don't I even get a word of thanks?<end>"
KO: "…나도 쓰러질 정도로 열심히 했는데. 나한테는 감사하다는 말 같은 건 없어?<end>"

## 1400120
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、あ、すみません！！<line-break>すぐに人を呼んできます。<end>"
EN: "O-oh, I'm sorry!! I'll fetch help at once.<end>"
KO: "아, 아, 죄송합니다!! 바로 사람을 불러오겠습니다.<end>"

## 1400121
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ありゃりゃ、本気にして<line-break>行っちゃったよ。<line-break>意地悪しちまったかな？<line-break>…にしても、システィーナの伝道師ね。<line-break>そんな奴らが暗躍してやがったとは、<line-break>いよいよ、油断がならねえな。<end>"
EN: "Well, look at that. She took me seriously and ran off. Maybe I was a little cruel. Still, missionaries of Sistina... If people like that have been scheming in the shadows, we really can't afford to let our guard down.<end>"
KO: "어이쿠, 진짜로 믿고 가 버렸네. 내가 좀 심술궂었나? …그건 그렇고 시스티나의 전도사라. 그런 놈들이 암약하고 있었다니 이제 정말 방심할 수 없겠군.<end>"

## 1400122
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いいえ！　私はここに残ります！<line-break>私には王女としての責任があります！<end>"
EN: "No! I'm staying here! I have a duty as a princess!<end>"
KO: "아니요! 저는 여기에 남겠습니다! 제게는 왕녀로서의 책임이 있습니다!<end>"

## 1400123
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "人間には生きる義務ってモノもある。<line-break>行け！<end>"
EN: "People also have a duty to live. Go!<end>"
KO: "인간에게는 살아갈 의무라는 것도 있다. 가라!<end>"

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400131

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400132

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400140
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "とにかく、じっとしていてください！<line-break>すぐに人を呼んで運ばせます。<end>"

## 1400141
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400142
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400143

## 1400145

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400153
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "어쨌든 가만히 계세요! 곧 사람을 불러 옮기게 하겠습니다.<end>"

## 1400154
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、姫さん、足、早いのな…。<line-break>もっと、トロいのかと思ってたよ。　　<line-break>カッコいい…じゃん。<line-break>…にしても、システィーナの伝道師ね。<line-break>そんな奴らが暗躍してやがったとは、<line-break>いよいよ、油断がならねえな。<end>"
EN: "Heh. You're pretty quick on your feet, Princess... I thought you'd be much slower. Not bad... Still, missionaries of Sistina... If people like that have been scheming in the shadows, we really can't afford to let our guard down.<end>"

## 1400155
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400157

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400175
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400176
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そら、今回の罠の仕掛け人のご登場だ。<line-break>どうやら俺たち<line-break>全員ハメられたみたいだな。<end>"
EN: "And here comes the one who set this whole trap. Looks like we all got played.<end>"
KO: "자, 이번 함정을 꾸민 장본인이 등장했군. 아무래도 우리 전부가 당한 모양이야.<end>"

## 1400177
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そ、そうなのですか…。<line-break>残念です…。<end>"
EN: "O-oh, I see... That's a shame...<end>"
KO: "그, 그런가요…. 아쉽네요….<end>"

## 1400178
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…とても、残念です。<line-break>もしかしたら、自分の運命が変わる、<line-break>そんな気がしていましたのに…。<end>"
EN: "…I'm very disappointed. I'd begun to feel that perhaps my fate might change…<end>"
KO: "…정말 아쉽네요. 혹시 제 운명이 바뀔지도 모른다는 느낌이 들었는데….<end>"

## 1400179
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、<value:$28>様…。<line-break>あ、あの、空中庭園まで<line-break>ご一緒していただけないでしょうか？<line-break>ゼネテス様からお手紙が来たのです。<line-break>空中庭園に来るようにと。ですが、<line-break>まだひとりで行く勇気がなくて…。<end>"
EN: "Oh, <value:$28>... Um, would you come with me to the Hanging Gardens? I received a letter from Xenetes asking me to come there. But I still don't have the courage to go alone...<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28> 님…. 저, 저기, 공중정원까지 함께 가 주실 수 없을까요? 제네테스 님에게서 편지가 왔어요. 공중정원으로 오라고요. 하지만 아직 혼자 갈 용기가 나지 않아서….<end>"

## 1400180
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400182
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Thank you. I'm sorry for asking such a strange favor. Well then, <value:$28>, let us go to the Hanging Gardens.<end>"
KO: "감사합니다. …이런 이상한 부탁을 해서 죄송해요. 그럼 <value:$28>님, 공중정원으로 가죠.<end>"

## 1400183

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400185

## 1400195
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "이거, 나한테 온 편지야. 읽어 볼래?<end>"

## 1400196
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様をお慕いもうしております。<line-break>空中庭園でお待ちしております。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　かしこ　　<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes, I adore you. I shall be waiting for you in the Hanging Gardens. Yours sincerely,<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님을 사모하고 있습니다. 공중정원에서 기다리겠습니다. 삼가.<end>"

## 1400197
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わ、私ではありません！<line-break>こ、こんなはしたないマネ…。<end>"
EN: "I-it wasn't me! I would never do anything so indecent...<end>"
KO: "저, 제가 아닙니다! 이, 이런 상스러운 짓은….<end>"

## 1400198
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…はしたない、ねえ？　<line-break>誰かが自分の純粋できれいな気持ちを<line-break>うち明けるのは、はしたなくないぜ。<line-break>ま、そんな誰かさんが、<line-break>存在すれば、の話だがな。<end>"
EN: "“Indecent,” eh? There's nothing indecent about someone confessing sincere, innocent feelings. Assuming, of course, that such a person actually exists.<end>"
KO: "…천박하다고? 누군가가 자신의 순수하고 아름다운 마음을 털어놓는 건 천박하지 않아. 뭐, 그런 누군가가 존재한다면 말이지.<end>"

## 1400199
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…どういうことです？<end>"
EN: "...What do you mean?<end>"
KO: "…무슨 뜻이죠?<end>"

## 1400200
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あんたは賢いから、この手紙を見て、<line-break>すぐ気がついたと思うけどな。<line-break>同じような手紙をもらってるんだろ？<line-break>つまり、<line-break>俺たちは、ハメられて、ここまで<line-break>おびき寄せられたってことさ。<end>"
EN: "You're clever. I figured you'd know as soon as you saw this letter. You got one like it, didn't you? In other words, someone set us up and lured us all the way out here.<end>"
KO: "너는 영리하니까 이 편지를 보고 바로 알아챘겠지. 비슷한 편지를 받았잖아? 그러니까 우리는 함정에 빠져 여기까지 유인당한 거야.<end>"

## 1400201
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテス？<end>"
EN: "...Xenetes?<end>"
KO: "…제네테스?<end>"

## 1400202
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "姫さん、この手紙を書いて、<line-break>俺に愛の告白をしようってのが<line-break>レムオンちゃんだと思うかい？<end>"
EN: "Princess, do you really think dear Lemghon wrote this letter to confess his love for me?<end>"
KO: "공주님, 이 편지를 쓰고 나한테 사랑 고백을 하려는 사람이 레무온이라고 생각해?<end>"

## 1400203
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おもしろい展開ですけれど<line-break>ありえませんわ。<line-break>　<line-break>つまり、レムオン様も<line-break>おびき寄せられたと。<end>"
EN: "An amusing turn of events, but quite impossible. So Lemghon was lured here as well.<end>"
KO: "흥미로운 전개이긴 하지만 있을 수 없는 일이군요. 즉, 레무온 님도 유인당했다는 거군요.<end>"

## 1400204
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "たぶんな。<line-break>俺には手紙の文面も予測できるぜ。<line-break>恋は盲目、とはよく言ったもんだ。<end>"
EN: "Probably. I can even guess what his letter said. They weren't lying when they said love is blind.<end>"
KO: "아마 그렇겠지. 난 편지 내용도 예상할 수 있어. 사랑은 눈을 멀게 한다는 말이 딱 맞군.<end>"

## 1400206
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "いったい何を言っている！？<line-break>どういうことだ！<line-break>説明しろ、ゼネテス！<end>"
EN: "What in the world are you talking about?! What is going on? Explain yourself, Xenetes!<end>"
KO: "대체 무슨 소리냐!? 무슨 뜻이지! 설명해라, 제네테스!<end>"

## 1400207
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "僕から説明させてもらうよ。<line-break>エリエナイ公レムオン、フフ。<end>"
EN: "Allow me to explain, Lemghon, Duke of Elienai. Heh.<end>"
KO: "내가 설명하지. 말도 안 되는 공작 레무온, 후후.<end>"

## 1400208
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうやら、<line-break>今回の罠の仕掛け人のご登場のようだ。<line-break>気をつけろ。<end>"
EN: "It seems the one behind this little trap has arrived. Stay sharp.<end>"
KO: "아무래도 이번 함정을 꾸민 자가 나타난 모양이군. 조심해.<end>"

## 1400209
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうですか…。<end>"
EN: "I understand...<end>"
KO: "그렇습니까….<end>"

## 1400210
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、私もまいりません。<line-break>…私ひとりで行ったら、<line-break>あの方に拒絶されそうで…。<end>"
EN: "Then I won't go either. If I went alone, I'm afraid I might be turned away...<end>"
KO: "그럼 저도 가지 않겠습니다. …저 혼자 가면 그분에게 거절당할 것 같아서….<end>"

## 1400211
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今日は、すみませんでした。<line-break>変なことをお願いして…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry about today. Asking you for such a strange favor...<end>"
KO: "오늘은 죄송했습니다. 이상한 부탁을 드려서….<end>"

## 1400212
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あ、ああ、<value:$28>か。<line-break>ティアナから手紙が来てな。<line-break>今から王宮に行くところなのだ。<end>"
EN: "Oh... <value:$28>. I got a letter from Tiana. I'm on my way to the palace now.<end>"
KO: "아, <value:$28>인가. 티아나에게 편지가 와서 지금 왕궁으로 가는 길이다.<end>"

## 1400213
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "…There is something I would like to discuss with you. I will be waiting for you in the Hanging Gardens today. Tiana Liu<end>"

## 1400219
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400220

## 1400221
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml

## 1400222
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゼネテス様をお慕いもうしております。<line-break>空中庭園でお待ちしております。<line-break>　　　　　　　　　　　　　かしこ　　<end>"
EN: "Lord Xenetes, I adore you. I shall be waiting for you in the Hanging Gardens. Yours sincerely,<end>"
KO: "제네테스 님을 사모하고 있습니다. 공중정원에서 기다리겠습니다. 삼가.<end>"

## 1400223
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だっ、誰が貴様などに<line-break>ラブレターを出すものか！！<end>"
EN: "W-Who would ever send a love letter to the likes of you?!<end>"
KO: "누, 누가 네놈 따위에게 러브레터를 보낸다는 거냐!!<end>"

## 1400224
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だ、エリエナイ公。<line-break>そんなに怒ってちゃダメだぜ。<line-break>もっと、人生楽しまないとな。<end>"
EN: "I'm joking, Duke Elienai. Don't get so worked up. You ought to enjoy life a little more.<end>"
KO: "농담이야, 에리에나이 공. 그렇게 화내면 안 되지. 인생을 좀 더 즐겨야 한다고.<end>"

## 1400225
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それよりも、エリエナイ公。<line-break><value:$28>は俺の大事な友だ<line-break>手を出すのはやめてもらおうか。<end>"
EN: "More importantly, Duke Elienai, <value:$28> is a dear friend of mine. Keep your hands off.<end>"
KO: "그보다 엘리에나이 공. <value:$28>은 내 소중한 친구다. 손대는 건 그만두시지.<end>"

## 1400226
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "手を出す、だと？<line-break>だ、誰が実の妹に手を出すか！<line-break>　<line-break>だいたい俺は、総司令殿とは違って<line-break>女性関係は潔癖なのだ。<line-break>そういう警告は無用だ。<end>"
EN: "Keep my hands off? W-Who would lay a hand on his own sister?! Besides, unlike our esteemed commander-in-chief, I am scrupulous in my dealings with women. I have no need of your warning.<end>"
KO: "손을 댄다고? 누, 누가 친여동생에게 손을 대나! 애초에 나는 총사령관님과 달리 여자관계는 결백하다. 그런 경고는 필요 없어.<end>"

## 1400227
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だからこそ、言ってるんだよ。<line-break>女の扱い方も心得ていないような奴に<line-break><value:$28>といる資格はない。<line-break>特に、手を伸ばせば届く幸せにさえ<line-break>あれこれ理屈をこねて、手を出さない。<line-break>憶病な手合いなら、なおさらだ。<end>"
EN: "That's exactly why I'm saying it. A man who doesn't even know how to treat a woman has no right to be with <value:$28>. And that goes double for a coward who makes one excuse after another rather than reach for happiness already within his grasp.<end>"
KO: "그래서 하는 말이야. 여자 다루는 법도 모르는 녀석에게 <value:$28>와 함께 있을 자격은 없어. 특히 손만 뻗으면 닿을 행복에도 이러쿵저러쿵 핑계를 대며 손을 내밀지 않는 겁쟁이라면 더더욱.<end>"

## 1400228
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…何が言いたい！？<end>"
EN: "...What are you trying to say?!<end>"
KO: "…무슨 말을 하고 싶은 거야!?<end>"

## 1400229
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…我が弟、<value:$28>よ。<line-break>そんなバカのそばに寄るんじゃない。<line-break>バカとなまけぐせがうつるぞ。<end>"
EN: "My younger brother, <value:$28>... Stay away from that fool. The stupidity and laziness might rub off on you.<end>"
KO: "…내 동생 <value:$28>아. 그런 바보 곁에 가지 마라. 바보와 게으름이 옮는다.<end>"

## 1400230
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…だとよ。じゃあ、<line-break>エリエナイ公のそばにいると<line-break>傲慢と仕事中毒がうつるぜ？<end>"
EN: "You heard him. So if you get too close to Duke Elienai, will the arrogance and workaholism rub off on you?<end>"
KO: "…그렇다네. 그럼 엘리에나이 공 옆에 있으면 오만함과 일중독이 옮겠군?<end>"

## 1400231
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "奴のそばにいると<line-break>その上、女癖の悪さもうつる。<end>"
EN: "Hang around him and his womanizing will rub off on you too.<end>"
KO: "그 녀석 곁에 있으면 여자 버릇 나쁜 것까지 옮는다.<end>"

## 1400232
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、エリエナイ公のそばにいると<line-break>女っ気のない、仕事仕事の<line-break>多忙で灰色の毎日がうつりそうだな。<end>"
EN: "Then stay around Duke Elienai and you'll end up with no women in your life, nothing but work, and one gray, busy day after another.<end>"
KO: "그럼 에리에나이 공 곁에 있으면 여자 기운 하나 없는, 일뿐인 바쁘고 잿빛인 나날이 옮아붙겠네.<end>"

## 1400233
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "う、うるさい！<end>"
EN: "S-Shut up!<end>"
KO: "시, 시끄러워!<end>"

## 1400234
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "冗談だ、エリエナイ公。<line-break>どうやら、お前さんも<line-break>おびき寄せられたようだな。<end>"
EN: "Only joking, Duke Elienai. Looks like you were lured here too.<end>"
KO: "농담이다, 에리에나이 공. 아무래도 자네도유인당한 모양이군.<end>"

## 1400236
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>様！<line-break>私、手紙をちゃんと<line-break>自分で読んでまいりました！<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>! I read the letter properly, all by myself!<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 님! 저, 편지를 제대로 제 손으로 읽고 왔습니다!<end>"

## 1400237
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ファーロス総司令。<line-break>どういうおつもりなのです？<line-break>こんな所に呼びだすなんて…。<end>"
EN: "Commander-in-Chief Pharos, what is the meaning of this? Summoning me to a place like this...<end>"
KO: "파로스 총사령관. 무슨 생각이십니까? 이런 곳으로 불러내다니….<end>"

## 1400238
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…<value:$28>様？<line-break>それにレムオン様も…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>...? And Lemghon as well...<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>님? 레무온 님도….<end>"

## 1400239
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ゼネテスに呼びだされた、だと？<end>"
EN: "You mean Xenetes summoned you here...?<end>"
KO: "…제네테스가 불렀다고?<end>"

## 1400240
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ってことは、お前さんは<line-break>ティアナに呼びだされたわけか。<line-break>フフ、かわいいとこがあるもんだな。<end>"
EN: "So Tiana was the one who summoned you. Heh. That's rather sweet.<end>"
KO: "…그럼 너는 티아나에게 불려 온 거구나. 후후, 귀여운 면도 있네.<end>"

## 1400241
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何が言いたい！<end>"
EN: "What are you trying to say?!<end>"
KO: "무슨 말을 하고 싶은 거야!<end>"

## 1400242
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だから、さっきから言ってるだろ？<line-break>俺たちは全員、<line-break>罠にハメられたんだって<line-break>そら、今回の罠の<line-break>仕掛け人のご登場だ。<line-break>詳しいことは奴から聞いてくれ。<end>"
EN: "I've been telling you all along, haven't I? We've all been caught in a trap. Look, here comes the one who set it. Get the details straight from the source.<end>"
KO: "그러니까 아까부터 말했잖아? 우리 모두가, 함정에 빠진 거라고. 자, 이번 함정의장본인이 등장했군. 자세한 건 녀석에게 물어봐.<end>"

## 1400243
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか…。<line-break>ならば、俺も行くまい。<line-break>お前抜きでティアナには会わない。<end>"
EN: "All right... Then I won't go either. I won't meet Tiana without you.<end>"
KO: "그런가…. 그렇다면 나도 가지 않겠다. 네가 없는데 티아나를 만나진 않아.<end>"

## 1400244
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…うるさい。<line-break>行かぬと言ったら行かぬのだ。<end>"
EN: "...Enough. I said I'm not going, and that's final.<end>"
KO: "…시끄럽다. 안 간다고 했으면 안 가는 거다.<end>"

## 1400255

jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　招かれざる客が来たみたいだね。　　　<line-break>　　　　　彼女を追ってきたのかな？<end>"
EN: "Looks like we have an uninvited guest. Did you come after her?<end>"
KO: "초대받지 않은 손님이 온 모양이네. 그녀를 쫓아온 걸까?<end>"

## 1400256

source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml

EN: "Shalli!? Where are you!!<end>"
KO: "샤리인가!? 어디 있느냐!!<end>"

## 1400258
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Oh! This could be the classic setup! Shalli's talking to us from somewhere!<end>"
KO: "아앗, 약속된 전개일지도! 어디선가 샤리가 말을 걸고 있는 거구나!<end>"

## 1400259
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

KO: "샤리구나!! 젠장! 어디 있냐!!<end>"

## 1400260
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400262
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

## 1400263
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "シャリ！！<line-break>どこだ！　姿を現せ！<end>"
EN: "Shalli!! Where are you? Show yourself!<end>"

## 1400264
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よぉぉ！　偉そうに！<line-break>出てきなさいよ！　シャリ！！<end>"

KO: "뭐야아아! 잘난 척하긴! 나와, 샤리!!<end>"

## 1400265
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes

EN: "Shalli!! Where are you!? Come on out!!<end>"
KO: "샤리!! 어디야!? 나와!!<end>"

## 1400266
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その声…シャリだね！！<line-break>出てきなよ！<end>"
EN: "That voice... It's Shalli!! Come on out!<end>"

## 1400267
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　アハハッ、そんなに　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　いきりたたないでよ。<line-break>　　　　今日は特別な日なんだからさ。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　だから、招かれざる客だけど<line-break>　　　　　特別に、歴史の目撃者に<line-break>　　　　　　　してあげるよ。<end>"
EN: "Ahaha! Don't get so worked up. Today is a very special day. So even though you're an uninvited guest, I'll make an exception and let you witness history.<end>"
KO: "아하하, 그렇게 흥분하지 마. 오늘은 특별한 날이니까. 초대받지 않은 손님이지만 특별히 역사의 목격자로 만들어 줄게.<end>"

## 1400268
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　今、いにしえの魔道文明の大いなる遺産、<line-break>　　　　　　空中都市ラドラスが、<line-break>　　　　４人の巫女の力でよみがえり、<line-break>　　　　　大空へと飛び立つのさ！！<end>"
EN: "At this very moment, the Floating City Ladras, the great legacy of an ancient magical civilization, is being revived by the power of four priestesses and taking flight into the heavens!<end>"
KO: "지금 고대 마도문명의 위대한 유산, 공중도시 라드라스가 네 무녀의 힘으로 되살아나 하늘로 날아오른다!!<end>"

## 1400269
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　この空中都市ラドラスってね、　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　　こう見えて実は<line-break>　　　ものすごく大掛かりな兵器なんだよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　それこそ、これひとつで<line-break>　　　この大陸が沈んじゃうくらいのね。<end>"
EN: "You see, despite appearances, Floating City Ladras is actually an immense weapon―powerful enough to sink this entire continent all by itself.<end>"
KO: "이 공중도시 라드라스 말이야, 이렇게 보여도 실은 엄청나게 거대한 병기야. 이거 하나만으로 이 대륙이 가라앉을 정도로 말이지.<end>"

## 1400270
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　…そうだ！！　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　　それ、やっちゃおう！<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　この都市の浮上を願いながら、<line-break>　　　それでも死ななければならなかった<line-break>　　　　多くの悲しい魂たちのために！<end>"
EN: "...I know! Let's do that! For all those sorrowful souls who longed to see this city rise, yet had to die all the same!<end>"
KO: "…그래!! 그거 하자! 이 도시가 떠오르길 바라면서도 죽어야 했던 수많은 슬픈 영혼들을 위해서!<end>"

## 1400271
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　今生きるすべての命も、　　　　　<line-break>　　　　彼らの所へ連れて行ってやろう。<line-break>　　　さびしくないようにね。アハハハハ！<end>"
EN: "I'll send every living soul to join them, too. Then they won't be lonely. Ahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "지금 살아 있는 모든 생명도 그들이 있는 곳으로 데려가 주마. 외롭지 않도록 말이지. 아하하하하!<end>"

## 1400272
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　あ、もし、僕に　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　ご意見ご要望があるなら<line-break>　　　　この都市の制御の間まで来てよ。<line-break>　<line-break>　…って、言わなくても君はここまで来るか。<line-break>じゃ、今、そこと、この制御の間を直結するよ。<line-break>　　さ、こっちに来なよ、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Oh, and if you have any comments or requests for me, come to the city's control room. ...Though I suppose you'd come all this way even if I didn't ask. So I'll connect your location directly to the control room right now. Come on over, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아, 혹시 내게 의견이나 요구가 있으면 이 도시의 제어실로 와. …라고 안 해도 넌 여기까지 오겠지. 그럼 지금 그곳과 이 제어실을 바로 연결할게. 자, 이쪽으로 와, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1400273
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やあ、<value:$28>。<line-break>こんな時にまで君と一緒なんて<line-break>もうこれは、運命的な何かを感じるね。<line-break>ところで、これ、すごいんだよ。<line-break>一度巫女たちが起動させれば、<line-break>僕でも動かせるようになってるんだ。<end>"
EN: "Hello, <value:$28>. To think we'd end up together even at a time like this... It almost feels like fate, doesn't it? Anyway, this thing is amazing. Once the priestesses activate it, even I can operate it.<end>"
KO: "안녕, <value:$28>. 이런 때까지 너와 함께라니 정말 운명 같은 무언가를 느끼게 되네. 그런데 이거 대단해. 한 번 무녀들이 기동시키고 나면 나도 움직일 수 있게 되어 있어.<end>"

## 1400274
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2シャリ！！<line-break>巫女たちはどこにいるの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>3巫女は…<line-break>イークレムンはどこにおる！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>4一度巫女たちが…って、<line-break>巫女たちはどこにいるの？<end><if><value:$01><equal>5そ、そうだ、巫女だよ！！<line-break>いったいどこに隠しやがった！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…言え。<line-break>巫女たちをどこに隠した？<end><if><value:$01><equal>10あ、そうだ、巫女。<line-break>彼女たちはどこにいるんだよ！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>14そうじゃ、わしらは<line-break>その娘らを取り戻しに来たんじゃ。<line-break>どこにいるか言うのじゃ！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>15彼女たち、無事なんだろうね？<line-break>どこにいるんだい？　教えてよ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>17こっちはあんたなんかじゃなくて<line-break>巫女どもに会いたいのよ！<line-break>とっとと出しなさいよー！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>19お姉ちゃんたちをどこに隠したの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>11４人の巫女もまだ生きているのね？<line-break>どこなの…答えて！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>20……巫女は？<end><if><value:$01><equal>26シャリ、またもお主は…！<line-break>巫女様方をどこにやった！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>27４人の少女はどちらに隠された？<line-break>返答されよ、シャリ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193お、教えてください！<line-break>巫女さんたちは<line-break>ど、どこにいるんですか！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>191シャリ！！<line-break>巫女たちをどこにやったの！？<end><if><value:$01><equal>190巫女たちをどこに隠した！<line-break>答えろ！<end>その巫女たちに<line-break>会いにきたんだ。<line-break>どこにいるか教えなよ！<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2Shalli!! Where are the priestesses!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3The priestess... Where is Iiklmn!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>4“Once the priestesses have activated it”...? Where are they?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5Th-that's right, the priestesses!! Where the hell did you hide them!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>13Tell me. Where have you hidden the priestesses?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10Oh, right, the priestesses. Where are they!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Aye, we came to take those girls back. Tell us where they are!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15They're safe, aren't they? Where are they? Tell me!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17We came to see the priestesses, not you! Bring them out here, now!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19Where did you hide those ladies!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11The four priestesses are still alive, aren't they? Where are they...? Answer me!<end><if><value:$01><equal>20...The priestesses?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26Shalli, not again...! What have you done with the priestesses!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27Where have the four girls been hidden? Answer me, Shalli!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193P-please tell us! W-where are the priestesses!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191Shalli!! What have you done with the priestesses!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Where have you hidden the priestesses!? Answer me!<end>We came here to see those priestesses. Tell us where they are!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2샤리!! 무녀들은 어디 있어!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>3무녀는… 이크렘문은 어디 있느냐!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4한번 무녀들이… 아니, 무녀들은 어디 있어?<end><if><value:$01><equal>5그, 그래, 무녀들 말이야!! 대체 어디에 숨겼어!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13…말해라. 무녀들을 어디에 숨겼지?<end><if><value:$01><equal>10아, 그래, 무녀. 그 사람들은 어디 있어!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14그래, 우리는 그 처녀들을 되찾으러 왔다. 어디 있는지 말해라!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15그 사람들 무사한 거지? 어디 있어? 말해 줘!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17우린 너 따위가 아니라 무녀들을 만나고 싶은 거야! 얼른 내놓으라고ー!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19언니들을 어디에 숨겼어!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>11네 명의 무녀도 아직 살아 있는 거지? 어디야… 대답해!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>20……무녀는?<end><if><value:$01><equal>26샤리, 또 네놈이…! 무녀님들을 어디로 데려간 게냐!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>27네 명의 소녀는 어디에 숨겼나? 대답하라, 샤리!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193가, 가르쳐 주세요! 무녀분들은 어, 어디에 있나요!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>191샤리!! 무녀들을 어디로 데려간 거야!?<end><if><value:$01><equal>190무녀들을 어디에 숨겼어! 대답해!<end>그 무녀들을 만나러 온 거야. 어디 있는지 알려 줘!<end>"

## 1400275
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今、彼女たちには、<line-break>この都市の底にある動力の間で<line-break>動力になってもらってるよ。<end>"
EN: "Right now, they're serving as the power source in the power chamber at the bottom of the city.<end>"
KO: "지금 그녀들에게는 이 도시 밑바닥의 동력실에서 동력이 되어 달라고 하고 있어.<end>"

## 1400276
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "行かせないよ。<line-break>言ったでしょ？<line-break>君は招かれざる客だって。<line-break>僕はこれを使ってやるんだ。<line-break>それをジャマするのは<line-break>ほっとけないな。<end>"
EN: "I can't let you go. I told you, didn't I? You're an uninvited guest. I'm going to use this, and I can't have you getting in my way.<end>"
KO: "보내 줄 수 없어. 말했지? 넌 초대받지 않은 손님이라고. 나는 이걸 쓸 거야. 그걸 방해한다면 그냥 둘 수 없지.<end>"

## 1400277
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "全部終わったあとで<line-break>ちゃんと彼女たちも送ってあげるし<line-break>先に逝って待っててよ。<end>"
EN: "Once it's all over, I'll make sure to send them along too. Go on ahead and wait for them.<end>"
KO: "전부 끝난 뒤에는 그녀들도 제대로 보내 줄 테니까 먼저 가서 기다리고 있어.<end>"

## 1400278
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ…、意外にやるね。<line-break>まさか、これだけ力を出したのに　<line-break>負けるとは思わなかったな。<line-break>でも、たぶん君にとって<line-break>状況はちっともよくなってないよ。<end>"
EN: "Heh... You're better than I expected. I never thought I'd unleash that much power and still lose. But I doubt your situation has improved in the slightest.<end>"
KO: "후후… 의외로 제법이네. 이만큼 힘을 냈는데 질 줄은 몰랐어. 하지만 아마 네 상황은 전혀 나아지지 않았을 거야.<end>"

## 1400279
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ほ～ら、こんな所で暴れるから、<line-break>どっか壊れちゃったんだよ。<line-break>あーあ、もう空中都市も終わりかあ。<line-break>巫女や、砂漠の民のみなさんも<line-break>巻き添えにして墜落だ。<line-break>それだけじゃないよ。街に墜落したら…<end>"
EN: "See? You had to go rampaging around in here, and now something's broken. Oh, well. I suppose this is the end of the Floating City. It'll crash with the priestesses and all the people of the desert still aboard. And that's not all. If it crashes into a city...<end>"
KO: "봐, 이런 데서 날뛰니까 어디가 고장났잖아. 아아, 이제 공중도시도 끝이네. 무녀와 사막 사람들까지 휘말려 추락하겠군. 그것뿐만 아니야. 도시에 떨어지면….<end>"

## 1400280
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、ま、だいじょうぶかもね。<line-break>いざとなれば、墜落前に<line-break>竜王様が駆けつけてくれるから。<line-break>墜落して地上に被害が出ないよう、<line-break>これを君たちごと吹き飛ばしにさ。<line-break>じゃね、<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Still, maybe it'll be all right. If it comes to that, the Dragon King will arrive before it crashes. To keep the impact from harming anyone on the ground, he'll blast this whole place apart―with all of you inside. Bye, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "그래도 뭐, 괜찮을지도 모르지. 정 안 되면 추락하기 전에 용왕님이 달려와 주실 테니까. 지상에 피해가 안 가게 이걸 너희째로 날려 버리러 말이야. 그럼 안녕, <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1400281
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これが運命。<line-break>かきまわしたところで<line-break>悪く転ぶだけ。<end>"
EN: "This is fate. Meddling will only make things worse.<end>"
KO: "이게 운명이야. 휘저어 봐야 나쁘게 흘러갈 뿐이지.<end>"

## 1400282
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>………………。<line-break>…わかったわ。<line-break>…あなたの言葉に賭けてみるわ。<line-break>巫女は動力の間よ。行きましょう。<line-break>もう時間がない。急いで。<end>"
EN: "...All right. I'll take a chance on what you said. The priestesses are in the power chamber. Let's go. We don't have much time. Hurry.<end>"
KO: "………………. …알겠어. …네 말을 믿어 볼게. 무녀들은 동력실에 있어. 가자. 시간이 없어. 서둘러.<end>"

## 1400283
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>！<line-break>巫女たちの所へ行きましょう。<line-break>動力の間にいるって言ってたわ。<line-break>彼女たちなら<line-break>何とかしてくれるかもしれない。 <end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>、急ぐぞい！！<line-break>まずは、動力の間じゃ！　　　　　<line-break>　<line-break>そこで巫女たちを助け出したあと、<line-break>彼女らに爆発を防ぐ手立てを聞く。<line-break>これしかあるまい！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>17ああっ、もぅお！<line-break>どうしてこんなに矢継ぎ早に<line-break>絶体絶命が押し寄せるのよー！？<line-break>とにかく、なんとかするのよ！<line-break>まずは、捕まっている巫女たちに<line-break>話を聞くのよ！　急ぐのよー！！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>19大変だよぉ、<value:$28>！<line-break>爆発したら、みんな死んじゃうよ！<line-break>なんとかしなきゃ！！<line-break>そう言えばさっき、巫女の<line-break>お姉ちゃんたちが動力の間にいる<line-break>って言ってたよぉ。行かなきゃ！ <end><if><value:$01><equal>21<value:$28><line-break>まずは巫女たちを助けなければ。<line-break>動力の間だ！　急ぐぞ！！<end><if><value:$01><equal>27爆発を食い止めなければ！<line-break>まずは巫女たちを助けるぞ！<line-break><value:$28>。<line-break>動力の間だ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>26爆発を食い止めなければ！<line-break>急ぐのじゃ、<value:$28><line-break>動力の間じゃ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>193だ、大爆発に墜落！？<line-break>た、大変です！！<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さん！<line-break>早く巫女さんたちを助けないと！<end><if><value:$01><equal>191大爆発！？<line-break>冗談じゃないわ！<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>、急ぎましょ！<line-break>巫女たちを助けるしか<line-break>打開策はないわ！<end><if><value:$01><equal>190くっ！<line-break>何とか爆発を食い止めないと！<line-break>でもどうすれば…？<line-break>ラドラスの巫女である彼女たちなら<line-break>何か知っているかもしれない。<line-break>動力の間に急ごう！<end>大変だ！　早くなんとかしないと、<line-break>僕たちみんなドッカ～ンだよ！！<line-break>　<line-break>う～ん、う～ん…、そうだ！<line-break>まずは巫女のみんなを助けに行こう！<line-break>彼女たちなら何か対策知ってるかも！ EN: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>! Let's go to the priestesses. He said they're in the power chamber. They may be able to do something.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3We have no time to waste! Come, <value:$28>. To the power chamber!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4This could be really bad! I never imagined we'd be facing a crash and a huge explosion! First, let's get to the power chamber. If the priestesses are there, we'll have to beg them to help us!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5A-a huge explosion?! This is bad, <value:$28>! Not that there's much we can do about it... Wait, that's it! Maybe the priestesses can!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13You heard him. The priestesses are in the power chamber. Let's go. They're probably the only ones who can get us out of this.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10W-wait, Shalli! Where are the priestesses?! Oh, right... He said they were in the power chamber. All right, sis, that's where we're going!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14Hurry, <value:$28>! To the power chamber first! Once we've rescued the priestesses, we'll ask them how to prevent the explosion. It's our only choice!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15Let's go, <value:$28>. First we have to reach the power chamber and rescue the priestesses. Then we'll work with them to stop the explosion!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17Oh, come on! Why do we keep landing in one life-or-death crisis after another?! We have to do something! First, let's talk to the captive priestesses. Hurry!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19This is awful, <value:$28>! If it explodes, everybody will die! We have to do something! Oh, that's right―he said the priestess ladies were in the power chamber. We have to go!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21<value:$28>, we must rescue the priestesses first. They're in the power chamber! Hurry!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27We have to stop the explosion! First we'll rescue the priestesses. <value:$28>, to the power chamber!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26We must stop the explosion! Hurry, <value:$28>! To the power chamber!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193A crash and a h-huge explosion?! Th-this is terrible! <value:$28>! We have to rescue the priestesses, quickly!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191A huge explosion?! You have got to be joking! Hurry, <value:$28>! Our only hope is to rescue the priestesses!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190Ngh! We have to stop the explosion somehow! But how...? As the priestesses of Ladras, they may know something. Let's hurry to the power chamber!<end>This is bad! If we don't do something fast, we'll all go boom! Hmm... Hmm... I've got it! First, let's go rescue the priestesses. They might know how to stop this!<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$01><equal>2<value:$28>! 무녀들에게 가자. 동력실에 있다고 했어. 그들이라면 어떻게든 해 줄지 몰라.<end><if><value:$01><equal>3이러고 있을 때가 아니다! 간다, <value:$28>. 동력실이다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>4엄청난 사태일지도!! 추락해서 대폭발이라니! 일단 동력실로 가서 무녀들이 있다면 부탁해 도움받아야 해!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>5대, 대폭발!? 큰일이야, <value:$28>!! 우리 힘으론 안 되나… 그래! 무녀들이라면 어떻게든 할지도!<end><if><value:$01><equal>13들었지? 동력실에 무녀들이 있다. 간다. 이 상황을 타개할 수 있는 건 아마 그들뿐이다.<end><if><value:$01><equal>10자, 잠깐 샤리!! 무녀들은 어디 있어!! …아, 동력실에 있다고 했지. 좋아 누나, 거기다!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>14<value:$28>, 서두르자!! 우선 동력실이다! 무녀들을 구하고 폭발을 막을 방법을 묻는다. 이 수밖에 없다!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>15<value:$28>, 가자. 먼저 동력실의 무녀들을 구하고 그들과 힘을 합쳐 폭발을 막자!<end><if><value:$01><equal>17아아, 정말! 왜 이렇게 연달아 절체절명이 몰려오는 거야!? 아무튼 해결해! 먼저 붙잡힌 무녀들에게 물어보는 거야! 서둘러!<end><if><value:$01><equal>19큰일이야아, <value:$28>! 폭발하면 모두 죽어! 어떻게든 해야 해! 아까 무녀 언니들이 동력실에 있다고 했어. 가야 해!<end><if><value:$01><equal>21<value:$28>, 우선 무녀들을 구해야 한다. 동력실이다! 서두르자!!<end><if><value:$01><equal>27폭발을 막아야 해! 우선 무녀들을 구한다! <value:$28>. 동력실이다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>26폭발을 막아야 한다! 서둘러라, <value:$28>. 동력실이다!<end><if><value:$01><equal>193대, 대폭발에 추락!? 큰일이에요!! <value:$28> 씨! 빨리 무녀들을 구해야 해요!<end><if><value:$01><equal>191대폭발!? 장난 아니야! <value:$28>, 서두르자! 무녀들을 구하는 수밖에 없어!<end><if><value:$01><equal>190큭! 어떻게든 폭발을 막아야 해! 하지만 어떻게…? 라드라스 무녀라면 뭔가 알지 몰라. 동력실로 서두르자!<end>큰일이야! 빨리 해결하지 않으면 모두 펑이야!! 음… 그래! 먼저 무녀들을 구하러 가자! 뭔가 방법을 알지도 몰라!<end>"

## 1400284
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やれやれっと、<line-break>やっと、おとなしくしてくれる気に<line-break>なったみたいだね。<end>"
EN: "Well, well. Looks like you've finally decided to behave yourselves.<end>"
KO: "휴우, 드디어 얌전히 있을 생각이 든 모양이네.<end>"

## 1400285
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ん？<line-break>この振動、外から！？<line-break>来たね…。<end>"
EN: "Hm? That tremor came from outside!? The Dragon King is here...<end>"
KO: "응? 이 진동, 밖에서!? 왔군….<end>"

## 1400286
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　いく星霜をへて　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　なお消えぬ無念の思いより生まれし<line-break>　　　　　　　　虚無の子よ、<line-break>　　　　　ここはお前の世界ではない…。<end>"
EN: "Child of the Void, born of regret that has lingered undimmed through countless years, this is not your world...<end>"
KO: "오랜 세월이 흘러도 사라지지 않는 원념에서 태어난 허무의 아이여, 이곳은 네 세계가 아니다….<end>"

## 1400287
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お出ましだね…。<line-break>竜王…。<end>"
EN: "And here comes the Dragon King...<end>"
KO: "나타났군…. 용왕….<end>"

## 1400288
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　虚無の子よ…　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　この世界は汝を望まぬ…。<line-break>　　　　　　己があるべき場所へと<line-break>　　　　　　　　帰るがよい…。<end>"
EN: "Child of the Void... This world does not want you. Return to the place where you belong...<end>"
KO: "허무의 아이여… 이 세계는 그대를 원하지 않는다…. 그대가 있어야 할 곳으로 돌아가거라….<end>"

## 1400289
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "やんなっちゃうな、もう。<line-break>子供の遊びに出てきてほしくないね。　<line-break>　<line-break>それに、僕はこの世界の人たちの<line-break>満たされない想いから生まれたんだよ？<line-break>だから、ここにいたっていいじゃない。<end>"
EN: "Oh, this is getting tiresome. You really don't need to involve yourself in a child's game. Besides, I was born from the unfulfilled desires of the people of this world, you know? So there's nothing wrong with me being here.<end>"
KO: "정말 싫다니까. 어린애 놀이에 끼어들고 싶지 않네. 게다가 난 이 세계 사람들의 채워지지 않는 마음에서 태어났다고? 그러니까 여기 있어도 되잖아.<end>"

## 1400290
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにしても、<line-break>あいかわらずひどいことするなあ、　<line-break>竜王様は。<line-break>この空中都市の脅威を消すために、<line-break>ここの住人も巫女も、無限のソウルを<line-break>持つ者まで巻き添えにする？<end>"
EN: "Still, as cruel as ever, aren't you, Lord Dragon King? To eliminate the threat posed by this Floating City, you'll sacrifice its residents, the priestesses, and even the Bearer of the Infinite Soul along with it?<end>"
KO: "그나저나 여전히 너무하네, 용왕님도. 이 공중도시의 위협을 없애려고 여기 주민도 무녀도, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자까지 휘말리게 한다고?<end>"

## 1400291
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　　　笑止な。　　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　他人の心配をしてみせるか？<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>　　　　　　　汝は虚無の子、<line-break>　　　汝は痛みを知ることはできても<line-break>　　　　　　感じることはできぬ。<line-break>　　　ただのうつろな人形に過ぎぬのだ。<end>"
EN: "How absurd. You pretend to care for others? You are the Child of the Void. You may know what pain is, but you cannot feel it. You are nothing more than an empty puppet.<end>"
KO: "우습구나. 남 걱정을 해 보이느냐? 그대는 허무의 아이. 고통을 알 수는 있어도 느낄 수는 없다. 그저 텅 빈 인형에 지나지 않느니라.<end>"

## 1400292
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうだ。シャリは冷酷な人形だ<end>"
EN: "That's right. Shalli is a heartless puppet.<end>"
KO: "그래. 샤리는 냉혹한 인형이다<end>"

## 1400293
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違う。シャリは人形じゃない！<end>"
EN: "No. Shalli isn't a puppet!<end>"
KO: "아니야. 샤리는 인형이 아니야!<end>"

## 1400295
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "プッ…フフフフ！<line-break>なんの冗談？<line-break>アハハハハハ！<end>"
EN: "Pfft... Heh heh heh! What kind of joke is that? Ahahahaha!<end>"
KO: "풋… 후후후후! 무슨 농담이야? 아하하하하!<end>"

## 1400296
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フフ、あのね。僕は<line-break>すべての人の痛みを知ることができる。<line-break>だけど、痛みを感じることはできない。<line-break>自分の痛みすらね。<line-break>だからすべてに現実感がない。<line-break>本当にうつろな人形なんだよ。フフ…<end>"
EN: "Heh... Listen. I can know the pain of every person in the world, but I can't feel any of it. Not even my own pain. Nothing feels real to me. I truly am nothing but a hollow puppet. Heh...<end>"
KO: "…후후, 나는 모든 사람의 고통을 알 수 있어. 하지만 고통을 느낄 수는 없어. 내 고통조차도. 그래서 모든 것에 현실감이 없어. 정말 텅 빈 인형인 거야. 후후….<end>"

## 1400297
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それとも、今のは大いなる予言かな？<line-break>この僕が、痛みにおびえる<line-break>普通の人間になるっていう。アハハ！<end>"
EN: "Or was that some grand prophecy? That I'll become an ordinary human being, frightened of pain? Ahaha!<end>"
KO: "아니면 방금 건 위대한 예언인가? 이 내가 고통을 두려워하는 평범한 인간이 된다는. 아하하!<end>"

## 1400298
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break><line-break><line-break>誰かの、痛みを、感じること…<line-break>それが、僕の、願い…？<end>"
EN: "...To feel someone else's pain... Is that... what I wish for...?<end>"
KO: "………………. 누군가의 아픔을 느끼는 것… 그게 내 소원…?<end>"

## 1400299
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うわ、言ってくれるね。<line-break>確かにその通りだけどさ。<end>"
EN: "Wow. You really don't hold back, do you? Though I can't say you're wrong.<end>"
KO: "우와, 말은 잘하네. 확실히 맞는 말이긴 하지만.<end>"

## 1400300
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さてと、そろそろ潮時かな。<line-break>このオモチャが墜落する前に<line-break>僕は失礼するよ。<end>"
EN: "Well, I suppose it's time for me to go. I'll take my leave before this toy crashes.<end>"
KO: "자, 슬슬 떠날 때인가. 이 장난감이 추락하기 전에 난 실례하지.<end>"

## 1400301
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃ、<value:$28>。<line-break>せっかくだから、墜落の瞬間まで<line-break>ゆっくりしてってよ。<line-break>あはははは！<line-break>じゃあね～。<end>"
EN: "Well then, <value:$28>. Since you're here, why not relax and enjoy the moment of impact? Ahahahaha! Bye-bye!<end>"
KO: "그럼 <value:$28>. 모처럼이니 추락하는 순간까지 느긋하게 있다 가. 아하하하! 잘 가~.<end>"

## 1400302
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　…<value:$28>、<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ…　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　空中都市はこのままゆけば、<line-break>　　　　あとわずかの時で地に落ちよう。<end>"
EN: "...<value:$28>, Bearer of the Infinite Soul... If nothing is done, the Floating City will soon fall to the earth.<end>"
KO: "…<value:$28>, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여… 공중도시는 이대로라면 이제 얼마 지나지 않아 땅으로 추락할 것이다.<end>"

## 1400303
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　動力の間へ行き　　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　巫女たちを助け出すのだ。<line-break>　　巫女ならば最悪の事態を回避する術を<line-break>　　　　　　　持っていよう…。<line-break>　　　力及ばず虚無の子に敗れたとはいえ、<line-break>　　この空中都市の脅威を退けようとした<line-break>　　　　　　　その魂、見事なり。<line-break>　　　我が再び戦う力を授けてやろう。<end>"
EN: "Go to the power chamber and rescue the priestesses. They may know how to avert the worst... Though your strength was not enough and you fell to the Child of the Void, your soul, which sought to repel the threat posed by this Floating City, is admirable indeed. I shall grant you the power to fight once more.<end>"
KO: "동력의 방으로 가서 무녀들을 구해라. 무녀라면 최악의 사태를 피할 방법을 알고 있을 것이다…. 힘이 모자라 허무의 아이에게 패했다고는 하나, 이 공중도시의 위협을 물리치려 한 그 혼은 훌륭하다. 내가 다시 싸울 힘을 내려 주마.<end>"

## 1400304
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　　　では、さらばだ。　　　　　　　<line-break>　　　　　無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<end>"
EN: "And now, farewell, Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "그럼, 잘 가라. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여.<end>"
