## 1400315
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　動力部、不要生命体、混入<line-break>　自動排除反応……動作開始<line-break>排除終了条件……生命活動停止<end>"
EN: "Power unit: unwanted life-form intrusion. Automatic elimination response... initiating. Elimination end condition: cessation of life activity.<end>"
KO: "동력부, 불필요 생명체 혼입. 자동 배제 반응…… 작동 개시. 배제 종료 조건…… 생명 활동 정지.<end>"

## 1400316
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　 動力部、大破…<line-break>排除反応、自動終了…<line-break>動力部全機能…停止…<end>"
EN: "Power unit: critically damaged... Elimination response: automatically terminating... All power unit functions... ceasing...<end>"
KO: "동력부, 대파… 배제 반응, 자동 종료… 동력부 전 기능… 정지….<end>"

## 1400317
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…<line-break>あなたが…来てくださるなんて…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I never thought you would come for me...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 당신이… 와 주시다니….<end>"

## 1400318
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "また私は、自分の役目を<line-break>果たしきれませんでした。<line-break>道具に、なりきれませんでした…。<end>"
EN: "Once again, I failed to fulfill my role. I couldn't fully become a tool...<end>"
KO: "또 저는 제 역할을 끝까지 해내지 못했습니다. 도구가 되어 버리지 못했어요….<end>"

## 1400319
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたに問題はありません。<end>"
EN: "There is no problem with you.<end>"
KO: "당신에게 문제는 없습니다.<end>"

## 1400320
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私の…内部に…<line-break>問題があるのです<end>"
EN: "The problem... is inside me.<end>"
KO: "제… 내부에… 문제가 있습니다.<end>"

## 1400321
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0………………。<line-break>………………。<line-break>…むう。<line-break>世間にかような複雑な思いが<line-break>存在することは知っておったが<line-break>よもやこの身に起ころうとはな…。<end><value:$28>よ…。<line-break>そなたがここまで来ること、<line-break>このエアにも見通せなんだ。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者の未来は<line-break>誰にも見えぬということか。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0... ... Hrm. I knew such tangled feelings existed in this world, but never did I imagine I would experience them myself...<end><value:$28>... Even I, Er, did not foresee that you would come this far. Is the future of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul hidden from all eyes?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0………………. ………………. …음. 세상에 이토록 복잡한 마음이 존재한다는 건 알고 있었지만 설마 이 몸에게 일어날 줄이야….<end><value:$28>여…. 그대가 여기까지 올 줄은 이 에아도 내다보지 못했다. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자의 미래는 누구도 볼 수 없다는 것인가.<end>"

## 1400322
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>よ…。<line-break>そなたがここまで来ること、<line-break>このエアにも見通せなんだ。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者の未来は<line-break>誰にも見えぬということか。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Even I, Er, did not foresee that you would come this far. Is the future of the Bearer of the Infinite Soul hidden from all eyes?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>여…. 그대가 여기까지 올 줄은 이 에아도 내다보지 못했다. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자의 미래는 누구에게도 보이지 않는다는 건가.<end>"

## 1400323
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん。<line-break>来てくれたんですね…。<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>. You came... Thank you.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨. 와 주셨군요…. 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1400324
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "イークレムン殿！！　<line-break>無事であったか！<line-break><line-break>よかった。<line-break>本当に、よかった…。<end>"
EN: "Lady Iiklmn! Are you all right? Thank goodness. Truly... thank goodness.<end>"
KO: "이크레문 님!! 무사하셨군요! 다행입니다. 정말, 다행이에요….<end>"

## 1400325
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "お父…さ…<end>"
EN: "Fa...ther...<end>"
KO: "아버… 지…<end>"

## 1400326
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いえ<line-break>アンギルダン様も<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "No... Lord Angeerdan, thank you as well.<end>"
KO: "…아뇨. 앙길단 님도 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1400327
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私…ずっと考えてました。<line-break><value:$28>さんが助けてくれたら<line-break>どんなにうれしいだろうって。<end>"
EN: "All this time, I kept thinking how happy I would be if <value:$28> came to save me.<end>"
KO: "저… 계속 생각했어요. <value:$28>님이 도와주시면 얼마나 기쁠까 하고요.<end>"

## 1400328
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そして今、<value:$28>さんは<line-break>本当に助けに来てくださって…。<line-break>私、本当にうれしいです。<end>"
EN: "And now you really have come to save me, <value:$28>... I am so happy.<end>"
KO: "그리고 지금 <value:$28>님이 정말 구하러 와 주셔서…. 정말 기쁩니다.<end>"

## 1400330
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "役割を果たせなかった…<line-break>…私は…不要な存在<line-break>だから…消えて…いなくなる…。<end>"
EN: "I failed to fulfill my role... I am not needed. So I'll disappear... cease to exist...<end>"
KO: "역할을 다하지 못했어… 나는… 필요 없는 존재니까… 사라져… 없어질 거야….<end>"

## 1400331
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "このような所まで…。<line-break>まったく、そなたのことだけは<line-break>このわらわにも見通せぬわ。<line-break>しかし、見通せぬがゆえに<line-break>うれしさも、ひとしおではあるがな…。<end>"
EN: "To think you would come all this way... Truly, you alone remain beyond even my sight. Yet that only makes my joy the greater...<end>"
KO: "이런 곳까지… 정말 그대만큼은 나도 내다볼 수 없구나. 하지만 알 수 없기에 기쁨도 더욱 크다…<end>"

## 1400332
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あー、んー、あのー…<line-break>いい雰囲気なのはわかるんだけど<line-break>そろそろ起きあがってもいいかな？<end>"
EN: "Uh, well, um... I know this is a touching moment and all, but can I get up now?<end>"
KO: "아, 음, 저기…. 분위기 좋은 건 알겠는데 슬슬 일어나도 될까?<end>"

## 1400333
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "い、今さら、何しに来たんだよ。<line-break>ずっと、ずっと、いつだって<line-break>ボクはキミのことを待ってたのに…。<line-break>ホントに…。<line-break>来るのが遅すぎるんだよぉ…。<end>"
EN: "W-what are you doing here now? I waited for you. All that time, every single moment, I waited for you... Honestly... You took far too long...<end>"
KO: "이, 이제 와서 뭐 하러 온 거야. 계속, 계속, 언제나 난 널 기다렸는데… 정말… 너무 늦었잖아…<end>"

## 1400334
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…まあ、でも、<line-break>こうして来てくれたから、よし！<end>"
EN: "Well... you came in the end, so all is forgiven!<end>"
KO: "…뭐, 그래도 이렇게 와 줬으니 됐어!<end>"

## 1400336
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さすがに今回は<line-break>ものすっごく恐かったよ～。<line-break><line-break>ボク、ここでラドラスに<line-break>魔力吸いとられて…、<line-break>もうダメかと…あ！！<end>"
EN: "That was really, really scary! Ladras was draining my magic here, and I thought I was done for... Ah!!<end>"
KO: "이번엔 정말 엄청 무서웠어~. 여기서 라드라스에게 마력을 빨려서 이제 끝인가 했는데… 아!!<end>"

## 1400337
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな！<line-break>大丈夫！？<end>"
EN: "Everyone! Are you all right?!<end>"
KO: "다들! 괜찮아!?<end>"

## 1400338
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そなたに心配されるほど<line-break>弱くはないわ、地の巫女よ。<end>"
EN: "I am not so weak as to need your concern, Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "그대에게 걱정받을 만큼 약하지는 않다, 땅의 무녀여.<end>"

## 1400339
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "まだ少し、体に力が入りませんが…<line-break>大丈夫です。<value:$28>さん、<line-break>ありがとうございます。<end>"
EN: "I still feel a little weak, but I am all right. Thank you, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "아직 몸에 힘이 잘 들어가진 않지만… 괜찮습니다. <value:$28>님, 감사합니다.<end>"

## 1400340
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空中都市が落下してる…。<line-break>このままじゃ、一族のみんなも<line-break>ボクたちも終わりだ。<end>"
EN: "The Floating City is falling... At this rate, our entire clan―and all of us―will be finished.<end>"
KO: "공중도시가 추락하고 있어…. 이대로면 일족 모두도 우리도 끝이야.<end>"

## 1400341
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それにもし<line-break>空中都市が街に墜落すれば<line-break>それ以上の被害が出るだろう。<end>"
EN: "And if the Floating City crashes into a town, the devastation will be even worse.<end>"
KO: "게다가 공중도시가 도시에 추락하면 그보다 더 큰 피해가 날 거야.<end>"

## 1400343
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フッ、気軽に言うてくれるわ。<line-break>よかろう。制御の間へ転送しようぞ。<end>"
EN: "Hmph. You make it sound so easy. Very well. I shall teleport us to the control chamber.<end>"
KO: "흥, 쉽게 말하는군. 좋다. 제어의 방으로 전송해 주지.<end>"

## 1400344
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "みんな、協力してほしい。<line-break>みんなの魔力を集めれば<line-break>多人数の空間転移も可能なハズだ。<line-break>エア、イークレムン、フレアは<line-break><value:$28>と一族のみんなを<line-break>転移させてここから脱出して。<end>"
EN: "Everyone, I need your help. If we combine our magic, we should be able to teleport a large group. Er, Iiklmn, Flare―teleport <value:$28> and the entire clan away from here.<end>"
KO: "모두 협력해 줘. 모두의 마력을 모으면 많은 사람을 한꺼번에 공간 전이시키는 것도 가능할 거야. 에아, 이크레문, 플레어는 <value:$28>와 일족 모두를 전이시켜 여기서 탈출시켜 줘.<end>"

## 1400345
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "地の巫女のボクは<line-break>空中都市を魔力で操って<line-break>竜骨の砂漠に突っ込む。<end>"
EN: "As the Priestess of Earth, I'll use my magic to steer the Floating City into the Dragonbone Desert.<end>"
KO: "땅의 무녀인 나는 공중도시를 마력으로 움직여 용골 사막에 들이받을 거야.<end>"

## 1400346
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よいのです、<line-break><value:$28>様。<end>"
EN: "It is all right, <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "괜찮습니다, <value:$28>님.<end>"

## 1400347
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "よーし！<line-break>じゃ、行くよ！<end>"
EN: "All right! Let's go!<end>"
KO: "좋아! 그럼 간다!<end>"

## 1400349
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "これ、結構、<line-break>集中しなきゃいけないんだ。<line-break>悪いけど、出て行ってくれる？<end>"
EN: "This takes a lot of concentration. Sorry, but could you all leave?<end>"
KO: "이거 꽤 집중해야 하거든. 미안하지만 나가 줄래?<end>"

## 1400350
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかりました。<line-break>あなたのお言葉に従いましょう。<line-break>エステル、地の巫女よ。<end>"
EN: "Very well. I shall do as you ask, Esther, Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "알겠습니다. 당신 말씀에 따르겠습니다. 에스텔, 땅의 무녀여.<end>"

## 1400351
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…ひどいよ、最後に振り返るなんて。<line-break>ボク、キミの方を見ないように<line-break>必死にガンバってたのに…。<line-break>ボク、キミの顔を見たら<line-break>絶対泣いちゃうじゃないか…。<line-break>決心が揺らいじゃうじゃないか…。<end>"
EN: "That's cruel... looking back at me at the very end. I was trying so hard not to look your way... If I see your face, I know I'll cry... I know my resolve will waver...<end>"
KO: "…너무해, 마지막에 돌아보다니. 난 너를 보지 않으려고 필사적으로 참았는데…. 네 얼굴을 보면 분명 울어 버리잖아…. 결심이 흔들리잖아….<end>"

## 1400352
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…エステル…さん。<end>"
EN: "Esther...<end>"
KO: "…에스텔… 씨.<end>"

## 1400353
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…フレア。<end>"
EN: "Flare...<end>"
KO: "…플레어.<end>"

## 1400354
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…このまま、空中都市の制御を<line-break>最後までやっていたら<line-break>あなたはここでガレキに埋もれます。<line-break>…死にますよ。<end>"
EN: "If you remain here and control the Floating City to the very end, you will be buried beneath the rubble. You will die.<end>"
KO: "…이대로 공중도시의 제어를 끝까지 계속하면 당신은 여기서 잔해에 깔립니다. …죽어요.<end>"

## 1400355
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "承知の上だよ。<line-break>一応、ボクだって巫女だもん。<line-break>わかってるよ。<end>"
EN: "I know. I am a priestess too, after all. I understand.<end>"
KO: "알고 있어. 나도 일단 무녀니까. 알고 있다고.<end>"

## 1400356
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あなたは必要とされています。<line-break>砂漠の一族の人々や<line-break>そして…<value:$28>に。<line-break>私なら、誰にも必要とされていません。<line-break>私が制御を代わります。<line-break>私は…死んでもかまいませんから。<end>"
EN: "You are needed―by the people of the desert clan, and by... <value:$28>. No one needs me. I will take over the controls. I do not mind... if I die.<end>"
KO: "당신은 필요한 사람입니다. 사막 일족 사람들에게도, 그리고… <value:$28>에게도. 저는 누구에게도 필요하지 않아요. 제가 제어를 대신하겠습니다. 저는… 죽어도 괜찮으니까요.<end>"

## 1400357
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "エステル…さん…。<line-break>どうして…<end>"
EN: "Esther... Why...?<end>"
KO: "에스텔… 씨…. 어째서…<end>"

## 1400358
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "だって、<line-break>キミには必要としてる誰かがいるから。<line-break>目を閉じてごらん、フレア。<end>"
EN: "Because there is someone you need. Close your eyes, Flare.<end>"
KO: "왜냐하면 널 필요로 하는 누군가가 있으니까. 눈을 감아 봐, 플레어.<end>"

## 1400359
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 1400360
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "誰かの顔が浮かんでこないかい？<line-break>誰かの名前が浮かんでこないかい？<end>"
EN: "Can you see someone's face? Does a name come to mind?<end>"
KO: "누군가의 얼굴이 떠오르지 않나? 누군가의 이름이 떠오르지 않나?<end>"

## 1400361
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes...<end>"
KO: "…네.<end>"

## 1400362
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その人がキミのこと必要としてる。<line-break>キミがその人のことを必要としてるのと<line-break>同じくらいに…。わかるよね、フレア。<end>"
EN: "That person needs you just as much as you need that person... You understand, don't you, Flare?<end>"
KO: "그 사람이 네가 필요해. 네가 그 사람을 필요로 하는 것만큼이나…. 알겠지, 플레어.<end>"

## 1400363
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "はい。<end>"
EN: "Yes.<end>"
KO: "네.<end>"

## 1400364
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、ボクもキミのこと好きだよ。<line-break>そして、キミに生きていてほしいと<line-break>切望してる。…それにね。<line-break>今、空中都市を制御し、破壊できるのは<line-break>地の巫女であるボクだけなんだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. I care about you too. And I desperately want you to live. Besides... right now, I'm the only one who can control and destroy the Floating City. I'm the Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "후후, 나도 네가 좋아. 그리고 네가 살아 있기를 간절히 바라고 있어. …게다가 말이야. 지금 공중도시를 제어하고 파괴할 수 있는 건 땅의 무녀인 나뿐이야.<end>"

## 1400365
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "フフ、そんなことはないよ。それにね。<line-break>今、空中都市を制御し、破壊できるのは<line-break>地の巫女であるボクだけなんだ。<end>"
EN: "Heh. That's not true. Besides... right now, I'm the only one who can control and destroy the Floating City. I'm the Priestess of Earth.<end>"
KO: "후후, 그렇지 않아. 그리고 말이야. 지금 공중도시를 제어하고 파괴할 수 있는 건 땅의 무녀인 나뿐이야.<end>"

## 1400366
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…そう、ですか。<end>"
EN: "I... see.<end>"
KO: "…그렇, 군요.<end>"

## 1400367
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さ、行って。<line-break>きっとエアもイークレムンも大変だ。<line-break>助けてあげて。<end>"
EN: "Now go. Er and Iiklmn must be struggling too. Go help them.<end>"
KO: "자, 가. 에아도 이크레문도 분명 힘들 거야. 도와줘.<end>"

## 1400368
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さよなら…フレア。<end>"
EN: "Goodbye... Flare.<end>"
KO: "…안녕… 플레어.<end>"

## 1400369
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボク、昔は、誰かのために<line-break>こんな砂漠に埋もれるのなんて<line-break>まっぴらだって思ってた。<line-break>それで、ラドラスを飛び出して…。<line-break>自分の族長って役割を嫌ってて…。<end>"
EN: "I used to hate the thought of being buried in this desert for someone else's sake. That's why I ran away from Ladras... I hated being clan chief...<end>"
KO: "나, 옛날에는 누군가를 위해 이런 사막에 묻히는 건 질색이라고 생각했어. 그래서 라드라스를 뛰쳐나와… 족장이라는 역할도 싫어했지….<end>"

## 1400370
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "でも、今、わかり始めてるんだ…。<line-break>ボクはここで埋もれる。<line-break>だけど…<line-break>だけど、ボクは、ここでこそ<line-break>生きているって言える…。<line-break>好きな誰かを守ることができる…。<end>"
EN: "But now I'm beginning to understand... I'll be buried here. But... this is where I can truly say I'm alive. I can protect someone I love...<end>"
KO: "하지만 지금 알기 시작했어…. 난 여기 묻히겠지. 그래도… 그래도 난 여기서야말로 살아 있다고 말할 수 있어…. 좋아하는 누군가를 지킬 수 있으니까…<end>"

## 1400371
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…さよなら…<value:$28>。<end>"
EN: "Goodbye... <value:$28>.<end>"
KO: "…안녕… <value:$28>.<end>"

## 1400384
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "……水の巫女様は<line-break>必要とされているのですね。<end>"
EN: "So... the Priestess of Water is needed.<end>"
KO: "……물의 무녀님은 필요로 하고 계시는군요.<end>"

## 1400385
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "空中都市が…竜骨の砂漠に…<line-break>完全に…、沈んだ…。<end>"
EN: "The Floating City... has sunk completely into the Dragonbone Desert.<end>"
KO: "공중도시가… 용골의 사막에… 완전히… 가라앉았어….<end>"

## 1400386
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん、<line-break>エステルさんは…<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, what about Esther...?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28> 씨, 에스텔 씨는…<end>"

## 1400387
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言わずともよい<line-break>水の巫女よ。<end>"
EN: "You need say no more, Priestess of Water.<end>"
KO: "말하지 않아도 된다, 물의 무녀여.<end>"

## 1400388
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "言わずとも、<line-break>無限のソウルの持ち主ならば<line-break>最良の結果を導こう。<end>"
EN: "There is no need to say it. The bearer of the Infinite Soul will bring about the best possible outcome.<end>"
KO: "말하지 않아도 무한의 소울을 지닌 자라면 최선의 결과를 이끌어내겠지.<end>"

## 1400389
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さて、精霊の巫女たちよ。<line-break>そなたらを、あるべき地へ<line-break>帰してやらねばなるまいな。<end>"
EN: "Now then, priestesses of the spirits. I suppose I must return you to where you belong.<end>"
KO: "자, 정령의 무녀들이여. 너희를 있어야 할 곳으로 돌려보내 줘야겠구나.<end>"

## 1400390
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "では、私は<line-break>アキュリュースへ帰ります。<line-break>　<line-break><value:$28>さん、<line-break>アキュリュースに来た時には<line-break>私の所にも来てくださいね。<end>"
EN: "Then I shall return to Aqyurius. <value:$28>, when you come to Aqyurius, please visit me as well.<end>"
KO: "그럼 저는 아큐류스로 돌아가겠습니다. <value:$28>님, 아큐류스에 오시면 제게도 들러 주세요.<end>"

## 1400391
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は…<line-break>道具としての役目を<line-break>完全に失ってしまいました…。<line-break>これから、どう生きればいいのか、<line-break>それすらもわかりません。<line-break>わかりません…しかし…<end>"
EN: "I have completely lost my purpose as a tool. I do not even know how I am to live from now on. I do not know... and yet...<end>"
KO: "저는… 도구로서의 역할을 완전히 잃었습니다… 앞으로 어떻게 살아야 하는지, 그것조차 모르겠습니다. 모르겠어요… 하지만…<end>"

## 1400392
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "なぜか生きていきたいと思うのです。<line-break>あなたと同じこの世界で生きること、<line-break>それがとても…何か…<line-break>理解できません。ですが感じるのです。<line-break>この感情は、苦しいのです。<line-break>しかし、何か…とても…<end>"
EN: "For some reason, I want to go on living. To live in this same world with you... it feels so... I do not know what it is. I cannot understand it. But I can feel it. This feeling is painful. And yet, there is something... something very...<end>"
KO: "왠지 살아가고 싶다고 생각합니다. 당신과 같은 이 세계에서 살아가는 것, 그것이 너무… 무언가…. 이해할 수 없습니다. 하지만 느껴집니다. 이 감정은 괴로워요. 그래도 무언가… 아주….<end>"

## 1400393
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…意味不明な言葉ばかり、すみません。<line-break><value:$28>様、<line-break>どうか、またお会いできますよう…。<end>"
EN: "I am sorry. I have spoken nothing but nonsense. <value:$28>, I pray that we may meet again...<end>"
KO: "…알 수 없는 말만 해서 죄송합니다. <value:$28>님, 부디 다시 만날 수 있기를….<end>"

## 1400394
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "さあ、そなたも行くがよい。<line-break>無限のソウルを持つ者よ。<line-break>　<line-break>最後に、礼を言うぞ。<line-break>そなたのおかげで、わらわも<line-break>未来に希望を見出せそうじゃ。<end>"
EN: "Now, you too must go, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. But first, you have my thanks. Because of you, it seems that even I may find hope in the future.<end>"
KO: "자, 그대도 가거라. 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 마지막으로 감사하마. 그대 덕분에 나도 미래에서 희망을 찾을 수 있을 것 같구나.<end>"

## 1400395
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "私は、ウルカーンへ戻ります。<line-break>そこにはもう、私の役目は<line-break>何もないのかもしれませんが…。<end>"
EN: "I shall return to Ulkarne. There may no longer be any purpose for me there, but...<end>"
KO: "저는 울칸으로 돌아가겠습니다. 그곳에는 이제 제 역할이 아무것도 없을지도 모르지만….<end>"

## 1400396
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>さん…、<line-break>本当にありがとうございました。<line-break>　<line-break>私…私もできることなら<line-break><value:$28>さんの旅に…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... thank you. Truly. If I could, I... I would join <value:$28> on the journey...<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>님… 정말 감사합니다. 저… 저도 가능하다면 <value:$28>님의 여행에….<end>"

## 1400397
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…いえ、なんでもありません。<line-break>私は、アキュリュースで<line-break>私のなすべきことをなしましょう。<line-break>では、<value:$28>さん、<line-break>また、アキュリュースへ…<line-break>私の所へいらしてください。<end>"
EN: "No, it is nothing. I shall do what I must in Aqyurius. Well then, <value:$28>, when you next come to Aqyurius... please come and see me.<end>"
KO: "…아니요, 아무것도 아닙니다. 저는 아큐류스에서 제가 해야 할 일을 하겠습니다. 그럼 <value:$28> 씨, 또 아큐류스로… 제게 와 주세요.<end>"

## 1400398
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>わらわは、今<line-break>希望を感じておる。<line-break>おそらく、わらわにとって<line-break>生まれて初めての希望じゃ。<line-break>未来を強く願うほどのな…。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... I feel hope now. Perhaps it is the first hope I have ever known―the kind that makes me yearn for the future.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>… 나는 지금 희망을 느끼고 있네. 아마 내게는 태어나 처음 느끼는 희망일 게야. 미래를 강하게 바라게 될 만큼…<end>"

## 1400399
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "今までのわらわは<line-break>見える未来に絶望するだけで<line-break>あらがう強さを持たなかった。<line-break>そなたが、教えてくれたのじゃ。<line-break>未来を切り開く強さを。<line-break>希望を持ち、進む生き方を…。<end>"
EN: "Until now, I could only despair at the future I foresaw. I had not the strength to defy it. You taught me that strength: the strength to carve out a future, to live with hope and press onward.<end>"
KO: "지금까지의 나는 보이는 미래에 절망할 뿐, 맞설 강함을 지니지 못했다. 네가 가르쳐 주었지. 미래를 개척하는 힘을. 희망을 품고 앞으로 나아가는 삶을….<end>"

## 1400400
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "………………。<line-break>　<line-break>　<line-break>行くがよい。<line-break>このままでは、わらわはそなたを<line-break>引きとめてしまいそうじゃ。<end>"
EN: "... Go now. If you remain, I fear I may try to keep you here.<end>"
KO: "………………. 가거라. 이대로라면 내가 그대를 붙잡아 둘 것 같구나.<end>"

JP: "追加メッセージここから<end>"
EN: ""
KO: "추가 메시지 여기부터<end>"

## 1400403
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　 　よくぞ、巨人パンタ・レイを倒した<line-break>　　　これで、我の力を妨げる者はない<line-break>　　礼を言うぞ。無限のソウルを持つ者よ<line-break><line-break>　　　　　我は感謝の証として、<line-break>　　　汝に我が力の一部を与えよう<line-break>　　人の子にはあまるほどの巨大な力を<end>"
EN: "You have done well to defeat the giant Panta Rei. Now none remain who can hinder my power. You have my thanks, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. As a token of my gratitude, I shall grant you a portion of my power―a vast power beyond the measure of any mortal.<end>"
KO: "거인 판타 레이를 잘 쓰러뜨렸구나. 이제 나의 힘을 방해할 자는 없다. 감사한다, 무한의 소울을 가진 자여. 감사의 증표로 그대에게 내 힘의 일부를 주겠다. 인간의 아이에게는 넘칠 만큼 거대한 힘을.<end>"

## 1400404
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "地の最高位の魔法が解放された。<end>"
EN: "The highest-level earth magic has been unlocked.<end>"
KO: "대지의 최고위 마법이 해방되었다.<end>"

## 1400405
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　これでエステルも解放されよう。　　　<line-break>　　　目覚めるがよい、地の巫女よ…。<end>"
EN: "Now Esther, too, shall be freed. Awaken, Priestess of Earth...<end>"
KO: "이제 에스텔도 해방되겠지. 눈을 떠라, 땅의 무녀여….<end>"

## 1400406
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28>…。<line-break>どうして…？<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>... Why?<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>…. 어째서…?<end>"

## 1400407
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　では、さらばだ<line-break>　無限のソウルを持つ者よ<end>"
EN: "And now, farewell, Bearer of the Infinite Soul.<end>"
KO: "그럼 안녕이다, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여.<end>"

## 1400408
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "どうして<line-break>パンタ・レイを倒したの？<end>"
EN: "Why did you defeat Panta Rhei?<end>"
KO: "왜 판타 레이를 쓰러뜨린 거야?<end>"

## 1400409
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ボクを助ける…ため…？<end>"
EN: "To... save me?<end>"
KO: "나를 구하려고…?<end>"

## 1400410
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28><line-break>キミはボクを<line-break>助けるべきじゃなかったんだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you shouldn't have saved me.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 넌 나를 구하지 말았어야 했어.<end>"

## 1400411
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "あのあと、ボクは<line-break>巫女として完全に覚醒した。<line-break>そしてエアのようにすべてを知った。<end>"
EN: "After that, I fully awakened as a priestess. And like Er, I came to know everything.<end>"
KO: "그 뒤 나는 무녀로서 완전히 각성했어. 그리고 에아처럼 모든 것을 알게 됐지.<end>"

## 1400412
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<value:$28><line-break>キミは…利用されていたんだ。<line-break>地の精霊神グラジェオンにね。<line-break>グラジェオンは闇の聖母ティラの子だ。<line-break>そして主神バイアスは巨人たちに命じて<line-break>闇の聖母神ティラを封じていたんだ。<end>"
EN: "<value:$28>, you were... being used. By Grajeon, the earth spirit god. Grajeon is the child of Tira, the Dark Mother. And Vyas, the Chief God, ordered the giants to seal away Tira, the Dark Mother Goddess.<end>"
KO: "<value:$28>, 너는… 이용당하고 있었어. 땅의 정령신 그라제온에게 말이야. 그라제온은 어둠의 성모 티라의 자식이야. 그리고 주신 바이아스는 거인들에게 명해 어둠의 성모신 티라를 봉인하고 있었지.<end>"

## 1400413
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "グラジェオンは母親を救おうと<line-break>キミを利用して、巨人を倒させ、<line-break>そして闇の聖母の封印を解いたんだ。<line-break>ボクは空中都市が落下したあと<line-break>この地の魔法力が乱れ、<line-break>封印が弱まってたのを守ってたんだ。<end>"
EN: "Grajeon used you to save Tira, the god's mother. You were made to defeat the giants and break the Dark Mother's seal. After the Floating City fell, the magical power in this land was thrown into turmoil and the seals weakened. I was guarding them.<end>"
KO: "그라제온은 어머니를 구하려고 너를 이용해 거인을 쓰러뜨리고 어둠의 성모의 봉인을 풀었어. 나는 공중도시가 떨어진 뒤 이 땅의 마력이 흐트러져 봉인이 약해진 걸 지키고 있었지.<end>"

## 1400414
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その１つを…キミは解いてしまった。<line-break>ティラの憎しみの強さは<line-break>知ってるよね。<line-break>キミは<line-break>ボクを助けるべきじゃ、<line-break>なかったのに…、だのに…。<end>"
EN: "And you... broke one of them. You know how deep Tira's hatred runs. You shouldn't have saved me. You shouldn't have... and yet...<end>"
KO: "그 하나를… 네가 풀어 버렸어. 티라의 증오가 얼마나 강한지 알잖아. 넌 날 도우면 안 됐는데… 그런데도….<end>"

## 1400415
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "それを…、キミが解いてしまった…。<line-break>ティラは世界を生み、<line-break>そしてノトゥーンに奪われた女神だ。<line-break>ティラは人が自然を破壊するのを<line-break>我が子が傷つけられるように<line-break>悲しみ、怒り、憎んでいる。<end>"
EN: "You... broke that seal. Tira is the goddess who created the world, only to have Notun take it from her. When humans destroy nature, she feels grief, anger, and hatred as though her own offspring were being harmed.<end>"
KO: "그 봉인을… 네가 풀어버렸어…. 티라는 세계를 낳고 노툰에게 빼앗긴 여신이야. 티라는 인간이 자연을 파괴하는 걸 자기 아이가 상처 입는 것처럼 슬퍼하고, 분노하고, 증오하고 있어.<end>"

## 1400416
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "その悲しみや怒りや憎しみが<line-break>神々の戦いを引き起こし、<line-break>それは、現在にまで尾を引いている。<line-break>かつて戦いに敗れたティラは<line-break>その身体を４つに裂かれて封印された。<line-break>キミはその封印を解いたんだ。<end>"
EN: "That grief, rage, and hatred sparked the war of the gods, and its effects linger to this day. Tira lost that war long ago. Her body was torn into four pieces, and each was sealed away. You broke that seal.<end>"
KO: "그 슬픔과 분노와 증오가 신들의 싸움을 일으켰고, 그 여파는 지금까지 이어지고 있어. 과거 싸움에서 패한 티라는 몸이 네 조각으로 찢겨 봉인되었지. 넌 그 봉인을 풀어버린 거야.<end>"

## 1400417
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "神代以来、破られたことのない封印を…<line-break>やがて、ティラは破壊神ウルグなど<line-break>比べものにならない災厄をもたらすよ。<line-break>キミは<line-break>ボクを助けるべきじゃ、<line-break>なかったのに…、だのに…。<end>"
EN: "A seal that had stood unbroken since the Age of the Gods... In time, Tira will bring a calamity that makes Ulugh, the God of Destruction, seem like nothing. You shouldn't have saved me. You shouldn't have... and yet...<end>"
KO: "신대 이래 한 번도 깨진 적 없는 봉인을…. 언젠가 티라는 파괴신 울그와는 비교도 안 될 재앙을 가져올 거야. 너는 나를 도와서는 안 됐는데… 그런데도….<end>"

## 1400418
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "バカだよ…。<line-break>本当にバカだよ、キミは…！<end>"
EN: "You fool... You really are a fool...!<end>"
KO: "바보야…. 정말 바보야, 너는…!<end>"

## 1400419
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ゴメン…。<line-break>ゴメン…。本当はうれしいです。<line-break>ゴメン…。ありがとう…。<end>"
EN: "I'm sorry... I'm sorry... The truth is, I am happy. I'm sorry... Thank you...<end>"
KO: "미안…. 미안해…. 사실은 기뻐. 미안…. 고마워….<end>"

## 1400420
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "（そして、たぶん、いつか、さよなら）<end>"
EN: "(And perhaps, someday, goodbye.)<end>"
KO: "(그리고 아마 언젠가는, 안녕)<end>"

## 1400421
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ううん…。<line-break>聞こえなくていいんだ。<line-break>今はね…。<end>"
EN: "No... It's all right if you didn't hear that. For now...<end>"
KO: "아니…. 안 들려도 괜찮아. 지금은….<end>"

## 1400422
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ね、また一緒に旅をしよう。<line-break>ボクを冒険に<line-break>連れていってよ。<end>"
EN: "Let's travel together again. Take me on another adventure.<end>"
KO: "있지, 다시 함께 여행하자. 나를 모험에 데려가 줘.<end>"

## 1400423
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うれしいよ…。<line-break>ボク、また、<line-break>キミの側にいられるんだね。<end>"
EN: "I'm so glad... I can be by your side again.<end>"
KO: "기뻐… 나, 또 네 곁에 있을 수 있구나.<end>"

## 1400424
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "じゃあ、また猫屋敷で<line-break>呼び出してくれるのを<line-break>待ってるから。<end>"
EN: "Then I'll wait at Cat Manor for you to summon me again.<end>"
KO: "그럼 다시 고양이 저택에서 불러 주길 기다릴게.<end>"

## 1400425
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "キミといっしょにいられる<line-break>この一瞬一瞬…<line-break>ボク…大事にするよ。<end>"
EN: "Every moment I get to spend with you... I'll treasure it.<end>"
KO: "너와 함께 있을 수 있는 이 순간순간… 나… 소중히 할게.<end>"

## 1400426
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　  　　　…来たか<line-break>　　   　無限のソウルを持つ者よ<line-break>　　　    我は汝を待っておった<line-break>　我こそは地の精霊神、グラジェオンなり<end>"
EN: "So, you have come, Bearer of the Infinite Soul. I have been awaiting you. I am Grajeon, Spirit God of Earth.<end>"
KO: "…왔는가, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여. 나는 너를 기다리고 있었다. 나는 대지의 정령신 그라제온이다.<end>"

## 1400427
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　  　　地の巫女、エステルは　　<line-break>空中都市崩壊時に発生せる魔法力場の嵐より<line-break>　  　 　　世界を守らんがため<line-break>　 この地にて、自ら封印となっておる<end>"
EN: "To protect the world from the magical force-field storm unleashed by the collapse of the Floating City, Esther, the Priestess of Earth, has made herself a seal here.<end>"
KO: "대지의 무녀 에스텔은 공중도시 붕괴 때 발생한 마력장의 폭풍으로부터 세계를 지키기 위해 이 땅에서 스스로 봉인이 되었다.<end>"

## 1400428
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　エステルを救う方法、なきにしもあらず　　<line-break>　　　　　我が力が完全に復活したならば<line-break>　　　　　力場の乱れ、収めてしんぜよう<line-break><line-break>　　　さすればエステルは封印となる必要なし<line-break>　　　　　再び汝とともに旅に立てよう<line-break>　　　　聖地母神の長兄たる我を封じる巨人<line-break>　　　　　　パンタ・レイを倒すがよい<end>"
EN: "There may yet be a way to save Esther. If my power were fully restored, I would quell the disturbance in the force field. Then Esther would no longer need to be the seal and could journey with you once more. I am the eldest brother of the Sacred Mother Goddess. Defeat Panta Rei, the giant who keeps me sealed away.<end>"
KO: "에스텔을 구할 방법이 아주 없는 것은 아니다. 내 힘이 완전히 되살아난다면 역장의 혼란을 가라앉혀 주마. 그러면 에스텔이 봉인이 될 필요도 없고 다시 너와 함께 여행할 수 있을 것이다. 성지모신의 장형인 나를 봉인한 거인 판타 레이를 쓰러뜨려라.<end>"

## 1400429
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "わかった<end>"
EN: "All right.<end>"
KO: "알았어<end>"

## 1400430
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…気が進まない<end>"
EN: "…I'd rather not.<end>"
KO: "…내키지 않아<end>"

## 1400431
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　　 よし　　<line-break>　　　 ならば、汝を<line-break>　　 精霊の座へ導かん<line-break><line-break>　　気高き地の光を越え<line-break>　 彼の巨人に挑むがよい<line-break> 挑み、そして打ち破るのだ<end>"
EN: "Very well. Then I shall lead you to the Seat of the Spirits. Pass beyond the noble light of earth and challenge that giant. Fight, and emerge victorious.<end>"
KO: "좋다. 그렇다면 너를 정령의 자리로 이끌겠다. 고결한 대지의 빛을 넘어 저 거인에게 도전하라. 도전하고, 쓰러뜨려라.<end>"

## 1400432
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　…そうか<line-break>　　無理強いはすまい<line-break><line-break><line-break>　うつろいやすき人の子よ<line-break>　　気が変わったならば<line-break>　 また我を訪ねるがよい<end>"
EN: "I see... I shall not compel you. Fickle child of man, should you change your mind, come before me again.<end>"
KO: "…그런가. 억지로 강요하진 않겠다. 변하기 쉬운 인간의 아이여, 마음이 바뀌면 다시 나를 찾아오거라.<end>"

## 1400433
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　来たか、無限のソウルを持つ者よ…<line-break>　　ならば、我はいま一度汝に問う<line-break><line-break><line-break>　　地の巫女、エステルを救うため<line-break>　　　強大な我が力を封じておる<line-break>　　　　巨人、パンタ・レイに<line-break>　　　　 　戦いを挑むか？<end>"
EN: "You have come, Bearer of the Infinite Soul... Then I shall ask you once more. To save Esther, the Priestess of Earth, will you challenge Panta Rei, the giant who has sealed away my mighty power?<end>"
KO: "왔는가, 무한의 소울을 지닌 자여…. 그렇다면 다시 묻겠다. 땅의 무녀 에스텔을 구하기 위해 나의 강대한 힘을 봉인한 거인 판타 레이에게 싸움을 걸겠는가?<end>"

## 1400434
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "しょうがない<end>"
EN: "I suppose I have no choice.<end>"
KO: "어쩔 수 없지<end>"

## 1400435
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "…気が進まない<end>"
EN: "…I'd rather not.<end>"
KO: "…내키지 않아<end>"

## 1400436
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　 　　よし　　<line-break>　　　 ならば、汝を<line-break>　　 精霊の座へ導かん<line-break><line-break>　　　気高き地の光を越え<line-break>　 　彼の巨人に挑むがよい<line-break>   挑み、そして打ち破るのだ<end>"
EN: "Very well. Then I shall lead you to the Seat of the Spirits. Pass beyond the noble light of earth and challenge that giant. Fight, and emerge victorious.<end>"
KO: "좋다. 그렇다면 너를 정령의 자리로 이끌겠다. 고결한 대지의 빛을 넘어 저 거인에게 도전하라. 도전하고, 쓰러뜨려라.<end>"

## 1400437
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "　　　　…そうか<line-break>　　無理強いはすまい<line-break><line-break><line-break>　うつろいやすき人の子よ<line-break>　　気が変わったならば<line-break>　 また我を訪ねるがよい<end>"
EN: "I see... I shall not compel you. Fickle child of man, should you change your mind, come before me again.<end>"
KO: "…그런가. 억지로 강요하진 않겠다. 변하기 쉬운 인간의 아이여, 마음이 바뀌면 다시 나를 찾아오거라.<end>"

## 1400754
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うーん。困った、困った。<end>"
EN: "Hmm. This is a problem. Quite a problem.<end>"
KO: "으음. 곤란하군, 곤란해.<end>"

## 1400755
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "おおっ、そのなりは冒険者だな。<line-break>ちょうどいい。<line-break>一つ頼みたいことがある。<end>"
EN: "Ah! That outfit marks you as an adventurer. Perfect timing. I have a favor to ask.<end>"
KO: "오오, 그 차림은 모험가로군. 마침 잘됐어. 한 가지 부탁할 게 있다.<end>"

## 1400756
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "うちの使用人が手違いでナジラネの果実を<line-break>余分に取ってきてしまったんだ。<end>"
EN: "A servant of mine accidentally gathered more Najirane Fruit than necessary.<end>"
KO: "우리 하인이 실수로 나지라네 열매를 너무 많이 따 와 버렸어.<end>"

## 1400757
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こいつは持っているだけで<line-break>沈黙状態になってしまう危険な品でな。<line-break> <line-break>こいつが生えているところ以外の場所に<line-break>捨てるとわしが罰せられてしまう。<end>"
EN: "It is a dangerous thing. Merely carrying it inflicts Silence. Yet if I dispose of it anywhere other than where it grows, I will be punished.<end>"
KO: "이건 가지고 있기만 해도 침묵 상태가 되는 위험한 물건이야. 자라는 곳 이외에 버리면 내가 벌을 받게 돼.<end>"

## 1400758
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "すまんが、こいつを賢者の森とかの<line-break>どこか安全なところに、<line-break>捨ててきてくれんかね。<line-break>もちろん、報酬は払う。<line-break>前払いで３００ギアでどうかね？<end>"
EN: "I am sorry to impose, but would you dispose of it somewhere safe, such as the Sage's Forest? You will be paid, of course. How does 300 Gea in advance sound?<end>"
KO: "미안하지만 이 녀석을 현자의 숲 같은 안전한 곳에 버리고 와 주지 않겠나. 물론 보수는 주지. 선불 300기어면 어떤가?<end>"

## 1400759
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受ける<end>"
EN: "Accept<end>"
KO: "수락한다<end>"

## 1400760
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "引き受けない<end>"
EN: "Decline<end>"
KO: "맡지 않는다<end>"

## 1400761
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、引き受けてくれるかね。<line-break>では、よろしく頼むぞ。<end>"
EN: "So, you'll take it on? Then I'm counting on you.<end>"
KO: "그런가, 맡아 주겠나. 그럼 잘 부탁하네.<end>"

## 1400762
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec140-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "そうか、困ったな…<line-break><end>"
EN: "I see. That is a problem...<end>"
KO: "그런가, 곤란하군….<end>"

## 1410002
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "こんにちは！<line-break><value:$28>さんの姿が見えたので<line-break>走ってきてしまいました。<end>"
EN: "Hello! I spotted you, <value:$28>, and couldn't help running over.<end>"
KO: "안녕하세요! <value:$28>님이 보여서 달려왔어요.<end>"

## 1410003
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0もう、ノエルったら<line-break>ひとりで走っていかないでよね。　<line-break>　<line-break>まぁ、ボウヤがいたんじゃ<line-break>仕方ないかしらね？<line-break>ノエルはボウヤにぞっこんだから。<end>もう、ノエルったら<line-break>ひとりで走っていかないでよね。　　　<line-break>　<line-break>まぁ、子猫ちゃんがいたんじゃ<line-break>仕方ないかしらね？<line-break>ノエルは子猫ちゃんにぞっこんだから。<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Honestly, Noel, don't go running off on your own. Though I suppose you couldn't help yourself with the boy here. Noel's completely smitten with him.<end>Honestly, Noel, don't go running off on your own. Though I suppose you couldn't help yourself with the little kitten here. Noel's completely smitten.<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0정말, 노엘도 참. 혼자 뛰어가지 말라니까. 뭐, 꼬마가 있었으니 어쩔 수 없었으려나? 노엘은 꼬마한테 푹 빠졌으니까.<end>정말, 노엘도 참. 혼자 뛰어가지 말라니까. 뭐, 아기고양이가 있었으니 어쩔 수 없었으려나? 노엘은 아기고양이한테 푹 빠졌으니까.<end>"

## 1410004
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "ちょ、ちょっと、カフィン…！<end>"
EN: "C-Coffin, wait...!<end>"
KO: "자, 잠깐, 카핀…!<end>"

## 1410006
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0ウフフ…あたしもボウヤに　　　　<line-break>この前のお礼をしなきゃ<line-break>いけないんだったわね。<line-break>さぁて、どんなお礼がいいかしら？<line-break>ねぇ？<end>そうだ。あたしも子猫ちゃんに　　<line-break>この前のお礼をしなきゃ<line-break>いけないんだったわね。<line-break>さぁて、どんなお礼がいいかしら？<line-break>ねぇ？<end>"
EN: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0Hee hee... I still owe you a proper thank-you for the other day, boy. Now, how should I thank you? Hmm?<end>That's right. I still owe you a proper thank-you for the other day, little kitten. Now, how should I thank you? Hmm?<end>"
KO: "<if><value:$29><equal>%0후후… 나도 꼬마한테 지난번 답례를 해야 했지. 자, 어떤 답례가 좋으려나? 응?<end>그래. 나도 아기고양이한테 지난번 답례를 해야 했지. 자, 어떤 답례가 좋으려나? 응?<end>"

## 1410007
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "カフィン、<line-break>子供をからかうもんじゃないよ。<end>"
EN: "Coffin, don't tease the child.<end>"
KO: "카핀, 아이를 놀리면 안 돼.<end>"

## 1410008
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "何よ、あんただって…<end>"
EN: "Oh, please. You were just...<end>"
KO: "뭐야, 너도…<end>"

## 1410010
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "な、何よ。<line-break>そんなに露骨に<line-break>あとずさらなくったって…<end>"
EN: "Wh-what? You don't have to back away quite so obviously...<end>"
KO: "뭐, 뭐야. 그렇게 노골적으로 뒤로 물러날 것까진 없잖아….<end>"

## 1410011
source: translations/korean/messages/msgsec141-part99.toml
jp_matches_pinned_english: yes
JP: "違う。<line-break>もうひとり、客が来た。<end>"
EN: "No. We have another visitor.<end>"
KO: "아니야. 손님이 한 명 더 왔어.<end>"
